# Tax Planning flow (client_tax_plans)

The master flow for tax planning engagements. Originally built as the **Tax Priorities** track inside VFO Holistic Planning (program_id=1); since aligned to serve the standalone **VFO Tax Planning** program (program_id=4) byte-equivalently — same handlers, same DB shape, same chains. The only intentional difference is the **Set Up phase** that program_id=4 plans see at the top (program_id=1 plans skip it because Holistic clients arrive via MAP1 instead). Parallel to [contract-and-payment.md](contract-and-payment.md) (MAP1) but operating on `client_tax_plans` rows. Touches every integration: Stripe, BoldSign, Gmail, Google Drive, Supabase Storage.

> **The ROI Meeting is shown as the TPOM (Tax Plan Orientation Meeting) — 2026-09-25, official from 2026-10-01.** DISPLAY ONLY: `src/components/shared/taxDisplayNames.js` maps the STORED phase `Tax 3 - ROI Meeting` and steps `ROI Meeting booked - Send Confirmation Email` / `ROI Presentation` to *Tax 3 - TPOM* / *TPOM booked - Send Confirmation Email* / *TPOM Presentation* on the Tax Planning tab (every portal) and the Client Overview phase + next-action columns. The stored names, which code in both repos matches exactly, are UNCHANGED, and so are the three bell TITLES *"Book the ROI meeting for…"*, *"Confirm the ROI meeting happened for…"* and *"Download the ROI presentation for…"* (a title is a clear contract, #411 — Jake's call). Handler messages, templates 16/217/219/227/228 and ten tax rule labels say TPOM. Everywhere below, "ROI meeting" names the same meeting; "ROI presentation" / "ROI deck" is still the deck's own name. See #540. **The same map (2026-09-25, DIRECT unit 3b) shows the Tax 2 step `Generate and download presentation` as *Generate TPOM Presentation*** (its green button reads **Open**; it still fires `tax_presentation_downloaded`, and the bell title *"Download the ROI presentation for…"* is unchanged) **and the Tax 5b step `Amend implementation fee` as *Amend fee*** (Tax 4's stored name already is). Both stored names stay lookup keys in both repos (`AMEND_IMPLEMENTATION_FEE_TASK_NAME`, `save-task.ts`, `tax-decision1.ts`).

The state machine is the column values on a single `client_tax_plans` row — see [../tables/tax.md](../tables/tax.md). Each step in this flow either updates the row or branches based on column values. `client_tax_plans.program_id` is set at plan creation by `tax_start_plan` (legacy rows pre-dating the column may be NULL — treated as Holistic for label purposes).

> **Program-aware client-visible labels** (added in the Tax Planning alignment session). The invoice/receipt PDF headers + footer, Stripe Checkout line items, the Stripe off-session implementation charge description, and the BoldSign agreement title all switch between "VFO Holistic Planning" (program_id=1 or NULL legacy) and "VFO Tax Planning" (program_id=4) via the `programLabel(programId)` helper in `utils/program-label.ts`. Internal-only labels (`notifications.pipeline='TAX'`, `email_templates.pipeline='TAX'`, sandbox-config `pipeline='TAX'`) stay program-agnostic — they're keys, not display names.

> **Session-3 redesign note (2026-05-19).** Steps 13 (Tax 4 Continue/Stop) and 14 (Tax 5 implementation) below describe the **original** Phase 6/7 designs where admin clicks fire money movement directly. Both have since been **redesigned** to mirror a unified pattern: admin picks a 3-option dropdown → for the affirmative pick, the client gets an email with a back-out button + 24h grace; for Undecided, the client gets a buttons-email with a 48h reminder + 96h PF-call-the-client admin notification; for the decline pick, the engagement closes immediately. **(Update 2026-07-22: the Tax 4 Continue 24h grace/auto-lock was REMOVED — Tax 4 Continue is now click-only with the 48h/96h nag ladder like Undecided; only Tax 5 implementation Proceed retains the 24h auto-charge — gotcha #264.)** **(Update 2026-08-14: the Tax 5 Proceed 24h auto-charge is REMOVED too. NOTHING on the tax track locks a decision or moves money without a client click; the Proceed branch now runs its own reminder ladder. In the same change every ladder tier on this page moved from calendar hours to **BUSINESS days** — read every "48h" below as **2 business days** and every "96h" as **4 business days**.)** Daily sweep (`tax-revshare-sweep-daily`) drives all timers. Same pattern was also added to Tax 3 (Undecided email reminder, agreement-signing reminder, payment-link reminder). For the canonical current behavior of these steps see [TAX_BUILD_RESUME.md](C:/vfo-edge-functions/.claude/worktrees/thirsty-gould-06a64e/TAX_BUILD_RESUME.md) Phase index and [../architecture/05-api-action-catalog.md](../architecture/05-api-action-catalog.md) entries `automation_TAX_postreviewdecision`, `automation_TAX_postreviewclientdecision`, `automation_TAX_implementdecision`, `automation_TAX_implementfinaldecision`, `automation_TAX_revshare_sweep`. Notifications now carry a `dismissible` boolean — FYI notifications have a Done button; action-required ones (Tax 3 Yes → pricing form; Tax 3 ExtraMeeting → schedule meeting) clear only when admin completes the action.

> **⚠️ FEE AMOUNTS MOVED OUT OF THIS DOC (2026-08-25). Read [tax-fee-process.md](tax-fee-process.md) before touching any amount.** From 2026-08-25 a NEW plan is priced from **one input (the total fee)** with every payable amount derived server-side, and a total above **$30,000** collects the retainer in **two payments** (an initial retainer — `$15,000` flat, or a custom amount for a client on the 2026-08-26 allowlist — plus a final), adding a whole money chain this page does not describe: the client's Client decision 1 click charges the final retainer off-session instead of releasing the revenue share, and the **retainer revshare is deferred** until that charge settles. Two new steps — **"Amend fee"** (Tax 4, before Client decision 1) and **"Amend implementation fee"** (Tax 5b, before the Implementation decision) — can re-price a signed engagement; they were admin-only until **2026-09-03 (v807)**, when both became **tax-planner-callable for both portal roles**, fenced by `denyIfNotPlannerPlan` (see [tax-fee-process.md](tax-fee-process.md) and #467), and a Tax 4 amendment to ≤ $30,000 **converts** a 3-payment plan back to 2 payments (reversibly). **The switch is `client_tax_plans.fee_process_version`: NULL = legacy, and no revised-process PRICING, column or validation ever reaches a legacy plan. TWO exceptions, both worth knowing before you read a step below.** *(2026-08-28)* a legacy plan's Client decision 1 green click now also drafts the `TAX_postreview_confirmed` acknowledgement email — an email that was previously missing, on the 2-payment wording (see step 13). *(2026-09-01, v802/v803)* **both amend steps are now REAL on a legacy plan inside its amendment window**, and they BLOCK the client decision the same way they do on a revised-process plan — the whole movement lands on `implementation_amount`, no charge fires, and the rev-share legs re-scale. A legacy plan already past that window renders exactly as it did before. See *[The Client Overview step machine](#the-client-overview-step-machine--utilstax-plan-stepsts-owners--forks-2026-08-18)* below and [tax-fee-process.md](tax-fee-process.md). The step-by-step narrative on this page is otherwise unchanged; the fee doc says which steps gained a fork and why.

## Lifecycle overview

```
ROI Mtg booked   →  Tax 3 Decision  →  /tax-decide      →  PCADMIN pricing  →  BoldSign agreement  →  Stripe customer  →  /tax-pay  →  Stripe payment  →  Confirmation + Invoice/Receipt  →  Tax 4 Continue/Stop  →  Implementation charge
   (Tax 2)         (Yes / Und / No)    (Yes / No /         (after client            (client signs,        (after both                                       (Phase 6 — admin-driven)            (Phase 7 — fires ONLY
                                          ExtraMeeting)        picks Yes)             then CEO)             signatures)                                                                              on the client's own
                                                                                                                                                                                                       Proceed click)
```

### Phase cards vs stored phases (display, 2026-07-22)

The tax track stores **7 phases on Holistic (program 1)** and **8 on VFO Tax Planning (program 4)**, but renders **6** and **7** cards respectively, because the two stored phases named `Tax 5` — `Tax 5 - Education & DD (Specialist Allocation)` and `Tax 5 - Education & DD (Post Allocation)` — render as ONE card badged `5`, with each half a sub-section inside it (own status pill, own unlock gate, one shared notes thread). Badge numbers are derived from the phase NAME (`Set Up` → `S`, `Tax <n>` → `n`), never the render position, so badges read `1,2,3,4,5,6` on Holistic and `S,1,2,3,4,5,6` on Tax Planning and always match the card title.

**Do not rename the stored phases** to "fix" the numbering — the names are exact-string-matched in `actions/clients/overview-tax.ts` (`phaseReach`), at ~14 sites in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`, and are stored as data in `client_notes.phase_name`. See gotcha #260. Steps below still refer to the stored phase names (including "Tax 5a"/"Tax 5b" shorthand), which are unchanged.

### Per-specialist phases: Tax 5a and Tax 6 (2026-07-31, v683)

**TWO phases store their tasks per allocated specialist rather than once per plan** — `Tax 5 - Education & DD (Specialist Allocation)` and, since 2026-07-31, **`Tax 6 - Implementation`** (gotcha #311). Their progress rows carry a non-NULL `client_tax_progress.tax_specialist_id` (FE progress key `${task.id}_${spec.id}`), written through the ordinary `tax_save_task` with a `tax_specialist_id` — **no dedicated action**. For a Tax 6 task a plan-level (`tax_specialist_id` NULL) row is **dead data** no reader will surface; the eight that predated the cutover were backed up and deleted.

- **Admin UI.** The Tax 6 card body renders **one expandable box per allocated specialist** (cloned from the Tax 5 specialist-box style — name display-only, chip, ▼ caret, expand key `tax6_spec_<id>`, default-expanded until all four steps are answered), each containing the phase's four tasks. With nothing allocated it reads "No specialists allocated yet."
- **Chip precedence per box: `Stopped` > `Done` > `In progress · n/4` > `Not started`.** Any step `Stopped` wins outright; "answered" counts `Completed`, `Stopped` **and** `N/A`. The card pill counts **specialists × 4**, and the hero total/done counts add Tax 6 as a per-specialist term (it is deliberately excluded from the plan-level reduce so it is not double-counted).
- **Phase done requires ≥ 1 allocated specialist** AND every specialist having every non-auto task statused. A plan with no specialists can never read Done — a deliberate change from the old vacuously-true behavior.
- **Client overview (the step machine — `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts` since 2026-08-17; `actions/clients/overview-tax.ts` is a thin caller).** A `Tax 6 - Implementation` branch emits **one step per specialist**, labelled `<task> — <specialist>`, gated on `applicable: implReach`, and tagged with the **same `spec-<id>` scope as the Tax 5 steps** — so one specialist's Tax 5 + Tax 6 steps scan as a single sequence for skipped-step warnings and never warn across two different specialists. **As of 2026-08-18 both per-specialist phases also carry a `groupLabel`** (`"Specialist allocation"` / `"Tax 6"`): the steps are still emitted one per specialist, but `next_actions` names the **phase** instead, because N specialists move in parallel and singling out whichever row sorts first is misleading. The phases' own single-owner steps (the implementation decision, the System charge/receipt/revshare trio) are deliberately **not** collapsed — they are genuinely one thing.
- **Removing a specialist** drops the row optimistically in the UI and clears both the `spec_<id>` and `tax6_spec_<id>` expand keys plus that specialist's progress keys; the backend still deletes the progress rows before the specialist row (#305).

### The Tax 5 picker roster is Tax-Planning-ecosystem-only (2026-08-17)

The "+ Add Specialist" dropdown (both programs, both admin and planner mode) lists **only specialists assigned to the Tax Planning ecosystem** — membership is `vfo_ecosystem_assignments` rows with `name = 'Tax Planning'`, never `experts.ecosystem_content`. Options are labelled **`<tax short bio> - <name>`** (e.g. `Oil & Gas Drilling Funds - Todd Lofgren`) and sorted by that label, case-insensitively.

- **Bio rule** (mirrored in two places — change together): if `experts.ecosystem_content` is the NEW array shape, take every entry whose `ecosystem === 'Tax Planning'` with a non-empty `short_bio`, in array order, joined `" / "` (one specialist may legitimately have several Tax Planning entries). LEGACY rows (`ecosystem_content` null or the old object shape) have no per-ecosystem bio and fall back to the flat `experts.short_bio` — so a multi-ecosystem legacy row shows its whole flat bio (e.g. `IRA Bailout Plan | Premium Financing - Darren Sugiyama`). No bio → the bare name.
- **Admin mode** computes this in the FE: `taxSpecialistOptions` / `taxShortBio` in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` read the cached `load_data` payload's `experts` + `ecosystems` (`ClientDetail` now passes an `ecosystems` prop alongside `specialists`). If the assignment rows are missing it falls back to the unfiltered roster rather than an empty picker. **Admin OPTIONS are also Active-only as of 2026-08-17** — the admin `load_data` roster ships every status (Lost / Removed included) where the non-admin path already strips to Active, so `taxSpecialistOptions` filters to `status === 'Active'` (a missing status counts as Active, which is what keeps planner rows working). This narrows the picker ONLY: `expertBios` is still built off the FULL roster, so a specialist allocated before they went Lost keeps rendering their `<bio> - <name>` on the plan card.
- **Planner mode** gets it precomputed: `tax_planner_portal_experts` (`portal-experts.ts`) already filters to the ecosystem and returns `{id, name, tax_bio}`; the FE only formats `tax_bio ? "<tax_bio> - <name>" : name`. The payload stays minimal on purpose — raw `ecosystem_content` carries D&B/audit content.
- **"Custom" remains the escape hatch** for allocating a specialist outside the Tax Planning ecosystem (see below); it stays trailing and exempt from the dedupe.

### Allocating an off-directory specialist: "Custom" (2026-07-31, v683)

The specialist picker's permanent trailing option is **"Custom"** (module sentinel `OTHER_SPEC_VALUE = '__other__'`, exempt from the already-allocated dedupe). Selecting it reveals a **name input** (80-char cap) and **Add stays disabled until it is non-empty**; the request is `tax_add_specialist { tax_plan_id, other: true, custom_name }`.

- The server owns the label format: `add-specialist.ts` sanitizes the name (collapse whitespace → trim → strip a leading case-insensitive `"custom -"` so it cannot double → 80-char cap) and stores **`Custom - <name>`**.
- **`expert_id` stays NULL**, so the allocation points at no `experts` row and can never reach a member showroom or the public widget (#305 / #231 / #201).
- **An empty or absent `custom_name` still produces the legacy `Other #N (See notes)` max+1 numbering** — retained for API back-compat and for rows already in the wild. The UI can no longer reach that path, and **no live row carries an `Other #N` name** (the last one was converted to `Custom - Steven Cox` on 2026-07-31).
- Neither unlinked path dedupes — multiple custom allocations on one plan are legitimate.

### Completion dates on the tax track are DISPLAY-ONLY (both programs)

Every step's completion date in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` is read-only: the date is whatever the save recorded and cannot be hand-edited. The mechanism is a **module-level `StepDate` component that SHADOWS the shared editable control** in `components/shared/StepDate` — the import was removed, and the local version renders a muted span, ignoring the `onChange`/`disabled` props the call sites still pass. Every other tracking track (MAP 1, onboarding, PFT, PIP, Regular) still imports and uses the **editable** shared control, so do not "fix" the tax call sites by re-adding the import. "Allocate Team Member / Tax Planner" now shows its completion date too.

**Which column feeds that cell (2026-08-17).** Ordinary steps read `client_tax_progress.completed_date`. The three steps with **bespoke renderers** have no progress row and must name their stamp explicitly — and each had been reading a field that is not a completion date at all (gotcha **#406**):

| Step (`status_options`) | Completion cell reads | Never |
|---|---|---|
| `tax_hlm_confirm` | `tax4_meeting_confirm_email_sent_at` | `tax4_meeting_date` — that is the **future** meeting being booked |
| `tax_presentation_link` | `presentation_scheduled_at`, falling back to `presentation_email_sent_at` | `presentation_send_date` — the **scheduled** send day, which can be in the future |
| `tax_returns_request` | `tax_returns_received_at`, falling back to `tax_returns_requested_at` | requested-before-received: the step completes on **receipt** |

`tax_presentation_link` **pins to the scheduled stamp and does not move when the sweep sends**, because scheduling is the human's work on that step and the send that follows is the system's. That is **deliberately the inverse** of `overview-tax.ts`, which was flipped to match in the same change — keep the two in step (#339). *(Address note: every `overview-tax.ts` reference in this file means the step machine, which moved VERBATIM to `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts` on 2026-08-17 and is now shared with the planner portal — see "The Client Overview step machine" above.)* The two fallbacks exist only for rows predating their column; a `tax_hlm_confirm` plan dated through the retired `automation_TAX_save_meeting_date` has no stamp at all and correctly shows **blank** rather than a wrong date.

### Risk profile seeding (Holistic only, 2026-07-22)

On a **Holistic** plan, `tax_start_plan` copies the client's MAP 1 "Priorities Decided / Risk Profile" answer onto the new plan's Tax 1 "Client risk profile complete" task (`Completed + Risk N` → `Yes — Risk N — <Mindset>`). Creation-time only, never re-synced; `Completed + N/A` seeds nothing; program 4 clients are excluded (no MAP 1 track). Expect a new Holistic plan to open at `1 / N` with Tax 1 reading "In progress". See gotcha #261.

Each arrow is either:
- A user clicking something in the admin UI (`callApi(...)`),
- A token-link landing page (raw fetch, no session),
- A webhook (Stripe / BoldSign), or
- A server-to-server chain (admin-api → admin-api with service-role or admin-token auth).

---

## The Client Overview step machine — `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts` (owners + forks, 2026-08-18)

**One file decides a plan's step list, and it is SHARED.** `buildTaxPlanSteps()` was lifted verbatim out of `actions/clients/overview-tax.ts` on 2026-08-17 and now has **two consumers** — Client Overview (via that thin caller) and the tax planner portal's client list (`actions/tax-planners/portal-clients.ts`, which uses `taxPlanStepsComplete()` for its Active / Complete / Stopped sections). **Anything you change here lands on both surfaces**, which is exactly the third-mirror problem #339 exists to prevent — and the reason the 2026-08-18 work was kept strictly additive, leaving `taxPlanStepsComplete()`'s semantics untouched.

**The two "amend the fee" steps are applicable by a THREE-WAY rule (2026-08-25, rewritten 2026-09-01 / v802).** `program_client_tasks` rows **178/179** (`Amend fee`, `status_options='tax_amend_fee'`, Tax 4, between *Detailed tax plan presentation* and *Client decision 1*) and **180/181** (`Amend implementation fee`, `status_options='tax_amend_fee_tax5'`, Tax 5b, before *Implementation decision*) exist in **both** tax programs (#116). `buildTaxPlanSteps` matches them on the status_options sentinel and sets `applicable: reach && (newFeeProcess || answered || windowOpen)` — a revised-process plan always has them; a **LEGACY** plan has them only if it has already ANSWERED the step (progress row **or** `fee_amended_at_*` stamp) or its amendment WINDOW is still open (`!plan.post_review_decision` at Tax 4, `!plan.implementation_decision` at Tax 5, read as truthiness so `''` counts as undecided). Those are the same columns `actions/tax/amend-fee.ts` guards on, so *applicable* never outlives *answerable*. Where the rule answers no — a legacy plan past its window that never answered — `applicable: false`, exactly like the steps a skipped ROI meeting removes: `computeTrack` and `taxPlanStepsComplete` both drop such steps entirely, so **that plan's completeness, `next_action` and warnings are untouched**, which is the whole reason the window term exists (~22 production legacy plans would otherwise sit permanently one step short on a row nobody could action). `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` carries the identical rule in TWO places — `isAmendNotApplicable` and a local mirror inside `getPlanState` — and #339 means all three move together. Each step reads done from **either** its own `client_tax_progress` row **or** the `fee_amended_at_*` stamp, so a lost `save-task` after a successful amendment cannot leave the step reading outstanding while the money has already moved — the FE's `isTaskStatused` uses the same two-source rule (#339). The two task NAMES are deliberately different and live once, in `constants/tax-fee-process.ts`; sharing one would make the Tax 5 gate pass as soon as the Tax 4 step was answered. Full rule + the legacy amendment semantics: [tax-fee-process.md](tax-fee-process.md).

**Every step names an owner.** `Step.owner` is a display string: the **allocated planner by name** where one exists (passed in as `ctx.plannerName`, batch-resolved from `tax_planners`), otherwise a role — `Tray` (returns request, green/red light, HLM confirm, ready-for-Tax-3), `Tracy / Tray` (the `tax_planner_select` allocation), `Admin` (deposit, risk profile — **not** the two amend-fee steps, which are planner-owned on both routes since 2026-09-25), `Client`, `Anton (CEO)` (countersign), and `System` for the automated cascade steps nobody should be chased about. **PF-owned steps are the exception: they emit the `PF_OWNER` token `"__PF__"`, never a name**, because the engines run before `enrichClients` and cannot see `clients.assigned_pf` — `actions/clients/overview.ts` resolves the token afterwards, and it is the only place that may. Gotcha **#415**.

**One step is SYNTHETIC: *Generate detailed tax plan presentation* (2026-09-25, DIRECT unit 3b).** It is not a `program_client_tasks` row — the tab renders it itself (`renderBookendsRow`) directly above *Detailed tax plan presentation* — so `buildTaxPlanSteps` pushes it immediately before that step, with the same `applicable` window and owner `PLANNER`. It is **done when `generated_bookends_at` is set OR the Detailed step is already answered**, so every plan that finished Tax 4 before the step existed shows no skipped-step warning. While pending it is what Client Overview and the planner portal name as the next action. See **Step 12¾** below.

**Two fork windows, because `computeTrack` is positional.** A single positional `next_action` names whichever step sorts first and hides everything beside it, which is wrong whenever two people are genuinely owed work at once. `buildTaxPlanSteps` therefore returns **`parallelNext`**, and `next_actions` renders one line per lane:

| Window | Lanes | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **1 — post-review** | Tray: green/red light → risk profile → ready-for-Tax-3 booking · ∥ · planner+PF: assess form → generate deck → send presentation link | Opens once *"Tax planner review complete"* is answered and closes when the Tax 3 decision is entered. **If the `tax_planner_select` step is applicable-and-not-done the fork collapses to that one entry**, owned `Tracy / Tray` — with nobody holding the plan, neither lane can move, so allocation is the only real ask. **Suppressed entirely when `pastTax3`.** |
| **2 — post-Continue** | planner: *Client decision 2* · ∥ · client: *Revenue share for initial 50%* | Opens on `post_review_decision = continue`. |

Both windows are derived from the **already-built `Step` objects** (`applicable === false` steps drop out), so the ROI-skip routes and the program-1 / program-4 template differences need no branching of their own. Outranking both is the **team-member rule**: a plan held by a Team Member with **no Tax Planner beside them** and not past Tax 3 owes exactly one thing — *"Allocate a Tax Planner"* — because a Team Member never runs a plan, they hand it on (the same hand-off `allocate-planner.ts` already fires a bell for). Allocation is a **two-hop** step for them, and any deeper step list would be a lie however far the template happens to read. **Since the [two-slot split](#allocation-is-two-slots-2026-08-26-v793) the test is `!ctx.plannerName && ctx.teamMemberName`** and the next action's **owner is the team member BY NAME** rather than the generic `PLANNER` role token; the old `ctx.plannerRole === 'Team Member'` test is kept beside it as legacy safety for rows written before the migration ran (it still emits the role token). Both `overview-tax.ts` and the planner portal thread `teamMemberName` in — the batch name lookup resolves both slots in its one query.

**`pastTax3` — the branch is proven by evidence, not by the decision column.** `yesPath` used to test `tax_decision` alone. **16 plans carry `legacy_source='old-system'` with a NULL `tax_decision`**: the migration recorded the state each plan had reached without synthesising the decision that preceded it, so signed-and-paid plans deep in Tax 5 were reporting a **Tax 2** step as their next action. `yesPath` is now OR-ed with `pastTax3 = agreement_sent='Yes' || client_signed='Yes' || retainer_status='succeeded' || post_review_decision != null` — every one of which is only writable **after** the decision, so it cannot false-positive. **The broadening reaches the planner portal too**, since the util is shared: a migrated plan's completeness is now judged on the same evidence. `pastTax3` additionally **suppresses Window 1** (a plan factually past Tax 3 must never re-fork into lanes it has already walked) and **bounds the team-member rule**. Gotcha **#414**.

**Phase collapses.** The two per-specialist phases and Regular's MAP 5 set a `groupLabel`, so `next_actions` names the phase rather than one parallel row — see the Tax 5a/Tax 6 section above. Separately, the assess step's template name is shortened for display by `shortLabel()` (it strips the trailing `"(and enter presentation details)"`, which belongs to the form, not to a one-line overview row); **the `program_client_tasks` name itself is untouched**, and the Window-1 lane lookup strips it identically — change one without the other and the lane stops finding its own step.

---

## STEP PREREQUISITE GATING — "natural barriers" (2026-08-13, frontend-only, gotcha #390)

Until this shipped, every step on the tax track was independently clickable in any order. The process existed only in whoever remembered it — an admin could send a presentation link before the returns arrived, allocate a Team Member and then complete a review only a Tax Planner may run, or record a client decision that nothing had led up to. **`TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` now enforces the order in the UI.**

### The mechanism

`stepGate(task, phase)` returns `{ locked, hint }`, or `null` when a step is unrestricted. `renderTask` consults it **before** `renderTaskInner` and before the plannerMode wrapper. A locked step renders an **inert row** — full brightness (not dimmed), hollow dot, task name in normal ink, a red circle-slash `LockedIcon` where the control would sit, a gray 11px hint naming the blocking step, and an em-dash in the date column.

Steps are matched **sentinel-first, exact-name-as-fallback** (the file's convention). Each gate names only its **DIRECT** prerequisites — the chain is transitively safe without recursion, because an earlier lock keeps the earlier step unanswered.

> **This is choreography, not authorization.** A lock alone is **not backend enforcement**: unless a handler 400 is named for it, `tax_save_task` will still accept a write to a locked step from any caller entitled to call it. Never cite a lock as a security control. Same class as #353. The rows that DO have a boundary behind them are called out below (the risk profile before booking; the Tax 4 chain; the Client decision 1 gates).

### The three safety rules

1. **`isTaskStatused` remains the single owner of every done-signal**, including the sentinels whose truth lives on `client_tax_plans` rather than in `client_tax_progress` (`tax_hlm_confirm`→`tax4_meeting_date`, `tax_returns_request`→`tax_returns_received_at`, `tax_planner_select`→**`tax_planner_id` ALONE — see the correction below, this is the one sentinel where `isTaskStatused` does NOT match the step's own green dot**, `tax_generate_presentation`→`generated_presentation_at`, `tax_presentation_link`→`presentation_send_date`, `tax_refund`→Proceed-or-refunded, and *"Additional information required"*'s status-plus-received-stamp rule). Every predicate reads through it, so a gate can never disagree with the green dot beside it.
2. **`alreadyDone` short-circuits the gate**, so a step that has ever been answered always renders its normal editable control — history stays visible and correctable even if a prerequisite is later un-set. Per-specialist steps key on the composite `${task.id}_${spec.id}` progress key (which `isTaskStatused` does **not** handle), and `tax_implement_decision` additionally accepts the plan column, because legacy plans carry `implementation_decision` with no progress row.
3. **An absent task is vacuously satisfied.** `prereqDone` returns true on a lookup miss — Holistic (program 1) genuinely lacks the deposit and Green/Red Light steps, and gating on a task that does not exist would lock everything downstream forever.

### The chain

| Step | Locked until | Hint |
|------|--------------|------|
| `Request Tax Returns` | (P4) deposit `pi_` entered | `Enter the Stripe deposit payment (Set Up) first` |
| `Allocate Team Member / Tax Planner` | returns received (P4 also deposit) | `Waiting for tax returns to be received` |
| `Additional information required?` | returns received **and a Tax Planner allocated** | three-way: returns / planner / both |
| `Tax planner review complete` | *Additional information* done **and** a Tax Planner allocated | `Complete "Additional information required" first` |
| `ROI Meeting booked` (`tax_3_decision`) | review = **Proceed** — or review = **Stop** on ANY plan (booking overrides the stop recommendation, 2026-09-21) — **and** `Client risk profile complete` carrying a grade (2026-09-21) | `Complete "Tax planner review complete" first`, then `Set the "Client risk profile complete" step first` |
| `Assess tax planning opportunities` | review = **Proceed** | same |
| `Generate and download presentation` (shown as *Generate TPOM Presentation*) | review = **Proceed**; then its own bespoke hint takes over | chain hint, then `Submit the tax planning opportunities form first` |
| `Send presentation link` | ROI meeting booked **and** deck generated | `Book the ROI meeting and generate the presentation first` |
| `ROI Presentation` | **every** Tax 1 + Tax 2 step (the Red Light step is never part of it — a proceeding plan does not carry one) | `Complete every Tax 1 and Tax 2 step first` |
| `Client tax planning decision` | `ROI Presentation` answered **— or the ROI meeting skipped** (`!roiPresentationDone && !roiSkipped`). **`meeting_first`: instead waits on `Detailed tax plan presentation`** | `Complete the "TPOM Presentation" step first` / (mf) `Complete the "Detailed tax plan presentation" step first` |
| `Detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email` | **not declined** (tested FIRST), then the Tax 3 AI PC Admin cascade completed (**FE-only** — a server gate would block legacy reschedules). **`meeting_first`: unlocked outright** — the skip itself is the unlock, since this meeting precedes the decision and the money | `Tax planning was declined` / `Complete the Tax 3 "Automated steps" first` |
| *Generate detailed tax plan presentation* (FE row, not a task — 2026-09-25) | that confirmation email sent (`tax4_meeting_date`); then a risk grade on *Client risk profile complete*. **Server-enforced** (`generate-bookends.ts` 400s both) | `Complete the "Detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email" step first`, then `Set the Client risk profile step first` |
| `Detailed tax plan presentation` | `generated_bookends_at` set — **same on both routes**. **Server-enforced**: `save-task.ts` 400s the FIRST answer without it (same wording); editing or clearing an already-answered step is always allowed | `Complete the "Generate detailed tax plan presentation" step first` |
| `Amend fee` (Tax 4) | `Detailed tax plan presentation` answered. **Server-enforced** since 2026-09-25: `amend-fee.ts` (stage `tax4`, ahead of the `keep` short-circuit) 400s with the same wording; a program without the Detailed step is left ungated | `Complete the "Detailed tax plan presentation" step first` |
| `Client decision 1` | `Amend fee` answered (tested FIRST — server-enforced by `postreview-decision.ts`), then confirmation email **and** detailed presentation. **`meeting_first` adds the retainer**: once both meeting steps are done it stays locked until `tax3AipcDone` (that wait is tested before the amend step) | `Complete the "Amend fee" step first` / `Complete the "Detailed tax plan presentation" step first` / (mf) `Waiting for the client to complete signing and payment` |
| `Client decision 2` | the above **and Client decision 1 ANSWERED BY THE CLIENT** (2026-09-23 — see *Client decision 1 has two halves* below; server gate `decision1Blocker`) | `Complete the "Client decision 1" step first` until the meeting steps are done, then `Waiting for the client to answer Client decision 1` / `The client chose a refund at Client decision 1` |
| Tax 5a per-specialist steps | Client decision 1 **answered by the client** | the same two decision-1 hints |
| Tax 5b *Post Allocation* | Client decision 1 **answered by the client**, tested FIRST and directly (a Confirm-ready row saved before 2026-09-23 must not open it); then the unchanged `tax5bUnlocked` rule | while decision 1 is open the hint names the NEAREST step: *Amend fee* (stored `Amend implementation fee`) `Complete "Client decision 2" first`, *Implementation decision* `Complete the "Amend fee" step first`; then `Unlocks when "Confirm ready for implementation" is Yes or Undecided on any specialist` |
| Tax 6 (all four, per specialist) | Implementation decision **and** its AI PC Admin cascade — **or** any Tax 6 work already started (`tax6Started`) | `Locked until Implementation decision + AI PC Admin complete` |

**Never gated:** `Deposit Paid`, `Client risk profile complete`, `Allocate to VFO Specialist` / adding specialists, every `AI PC Admin` row, `auto` steps, and the steps `renderTaskInner` hides outright.

#### `Client risk profile complete` before `ROI Meeting booked` (2026-09-21) — a gate with a boundary behind it

On **every** tax plan (classic and Direct, program 1 and program 4) the Tax 1 step **`Client risk profile complete`** must be answered before the Tax 2 step **`ROI Meeting booked - Send Confirmation Email`** (`tax_3_decision`). The grade is what the ROI deck is built from — `generate-presentation.ts` 400s without it — so it is settled before the client is told when the meeting is, rather than after. The FE gate is the usual choreography (`locked: !chainOk || !riskProfileDone`, second hint `Set the "Client risk profile complete" step first`), and the **admin Skip button shares that row, so skipping waits on the risk profile too — deliberate**.

Like the Tax 4 chain rows marked **Server-enforced** in the table (2026-09-25), this one **is** enforced server-side: `actions/tax/ready-for-tax3.ts` reads the plan-level `client_tax_progress` row (`tax_specialist_id IS NULL`) for the `Client risk profile complete` task — the same `program_client_phases` lookup and the same `/Risk\s*[1-5]/` grade test `generate-presentation.ts` makes — and returns **400 `Complete the "Client risk profile complete" step before booking the ROI meeting`** when no grade is stored. **A decline is not gated**, the same asymmetry the allocation guard above it carries. Note the deliberate looseness on the FE: the tab's `riskProfileDone` tests `status.includes('Risk')` (it is a hint, and it is the test the generate-presentation card already used), while the boundary requires the grade digit.

> **"AI PC Admin" is DISPLAYED as "Automated steps" everywhere, from 2026-09-21.** The stored `program_client_tasks.name` is unchanged and every `=== 'AI PC Admin'` match in either repo stays exactly as it is — the name is a lookup key on both sides of the wire (#382). Only what a row PRINTS changes, in two places: `TASK_DISPLAY_LABELS` in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` (which both tax cards already print through, via `taskLabel` / `stepName`) and `displayLabel` at the emit in `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts` (which is where Client Overview and the planner portal get their labels). The same swap was made at the hardcoded headings on the other surfaces — PIP, Regular, PFT, Specialist onboarding, and the member's MSM tracking view — and in the three user-facing lock hints that named it. **MAP 1 has no "AI PC Admin" TASK at all** (its automated block is the *phase* `MAP 1 - PC Admin`), so `ClientTrackViewV2.jsx` needed no change.
>
> Its owner chip reads **Automated** (grey): `stepOwner` returns `'auto'` for that one name, so the AI PC Admin cards carry a chip while every other `auto` row stays chip-less. Chip colours were re-cut in the same change — **VFOS green** (`#1b9254`), **Client purple** (`#6f42c1`), Tax Team orange and Member blue unchanged, Automated grey (`var(--vfo-muted)`). The two cards' "waiting" pills became gate-style hints (circle-slash + `lockedHintStyle`): *Starts automatically after the "Client tax planning decision" step* (Tax 3) and *… after the "Implementation decision" step* (Tax 5b), so the row says why nothing is happening instead of only that nothing is.

### The escape hatches (deliberate — do not "tidy" them away)

A gate that locks a stop path makes a bell un-performable (#365/#367), so the stop routes stay walkable:

- **The Tax Plan Red Light row is NEVER row-locked.** Its one button — **Refund** on a deposit plan, **Stop tax planning** on a waived one — is the program-4 stop route and must stay reachable for a client who never provides information, i.e. exactly when the diagnostic chain can never complete. Since 2026-09-21 there is nothing else on the row to gate: the Proceed button is gone (`greenRedOk` was deleted with it).
- **`reviewStop` unlocks `ROI Meeting booked` on EVERY plan (2026-09-21).** It always did on program 1, whose Stop bell names that step's decline button as the stop route (#367), and on a waived program-4 plan since 5c; the deposit plans joined when the Red Light Proceed — their old override — was removed. Booking the meeting anyway over a Stop recommendation is a real override, and `ready-for-tax3.ts` already treats it as one by clearing the Stop bell. The chain half of the gate is now simply `diagnosticChain || reviewStop` with one hint; since 2026-09-21 it is AND-ed with `riskProfileDone`, which carries the second hint (see the risk-profile prerequisite above — that term is not an escape hatch and does lock the Stop-override path too).
- **`tax6Started`** keeps Tax 6 editable on any plan where per-specialist work already exists, so the gate cannot strand historic rows.

### A terminal outcome is not "incomplete" — the decline lock (#403, 2026-08-16, v747)

`Detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email` (`tax_hlm_confirm`) was gated `locked: !tax3AipcDone`, and **`tax3AipcDone` is deliberately TRUE on a decline** (`tax_decision === 'No' || tax_final_decision === 'No'`) because a No *is* the cascade's terminal state. The gate therefore unlocked exactly the plans that must never reach the step, while live mid-cascade plans locked correctly — invisible on every happy path. **The decline test now runs FIRST** and returns its own hint `Tax planning was declined`; only then does the completion test run. Because a lock is choreography, [`highlevel-meeting-confirm.ts`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/highlevel-meeting-confirm.ts) also **400s** `"Tax planning was declined for this plan"` off the same two columns — that is the boundary. Bell-strand check was clean: `TAX_planner_hlm_ready` fires only on the paid Yes track, so locking the step makes no action-required bell unperformable (#365).

### "Answered" is not "Proceed" (#391 — this shipped as a bug and was caught in testing)

The first build treated *"Tax planner review complete"* as a boolean. Choosing **Stop tax planning** therefore unlocked the whole forward half of the track — Assess, ROI-booked, the deck, and a **Proceed** button on Green/Red, i.e. precisely the decision the Stop had just refused. The predicate is now **`reviewProceed`** (status exactly `'Proceed with tax planning'`) and the old any-answer `reviewDone` was deleted so it cannot be reused. **An override after a Stop is done by flipping the review answer back to Proceed** — the step stays editable once answered.

### Surfaces

- **Member portal (`readOnly`) on a CLASSIC plan: fully exempt.** Members see zero lock rows and zero hints.
- **Member portal on a DIRECT plan (`directMode`, 2026-09-18): sees the gates on EVERY row** — their own steps and the tax-team steps alike, so the whole plan reads with one vocabulary; a team step whose prerequisites are met falls through to the same inert lock wrapper the planner portal uses (`pointer-events:none` + a `title` tooltip naming the step's OWNER via `lockHintFor(stepOwner(...))`: *Handled by the Tax Planning Team* / *Handled by VFO Services* / *Completed by the client* / *Not available on this view*). The member's editable set is `DIRECT_EDITABLE_TASKS` (the FE mirror of `save-task.ts DIRECT_MEMBER_EDITABLE` plus the non-`save_task` button steps; cross-repo contract, #339/#392) and `actFor(directMode)` rewrites every editable call to its `tax_direct_*` twin through `DIRECT_ACTION_MAP`; everything else is `memberLocked`.
- **Tax-planner portal (`plannerMode`): sees the locks**, on top of the existing per-step whitelist inert-wrapper — which is GENERIC (#516): `renderTask` wraps every non-whitelisted row in `pointer-events:none` with the same owner tooltip and draws no hint row; only `stepGate` renders the circle-slash + hint. Owner chips (**Member** blue / **VFOS** green / **Tax Team** orange / **Client** purple / **Automated** grey, `stepOwner(task, phase, direct)`) render identically on all three surfaces.
- **Display-only renames (#392):** `TASK_DISPLAY_LABELS` / `TASK_SUB_LABELS` rename three step names in the UI — the Red Light step (**"Tax Plan Red Light"** since 2026-09-21, sub-label *"Refund the deposit, or stop tax planning, if unable to proceed"*), the Assess step (parenthetical dropped) and *"Additional information required?"* (question mark added). **Stored `program_client_tasks.name` values are untouched and remain the cross-repo lookup keys**; bells and email chips still speak the long names.
- **One deliberate member-visible change:** Tax 5 *Post Allocation* stopped being a full block-out and became ordinary locked rows with the `PhasePill` always shown, so members now see those rows where they previously saw an empty section.

---

## Step 0 — Set Up phase (VFO Tax Planning program_id=4 only)

**Context:** standalone VFO Tax Planning clients pay a $500 deposit BEFORE the plan exists. **Since 2026-09-17 the deposit is collected inside the portal**: the member starts the client from the member portal Tax Planning tab (or emails the client a link to the public `/tax-intake` page), the $500 is taken on a Stripe Checkout, and the webhook-driven `finalizeTaxIntake` creates the enrollment, the client, this plan and stamps this step — the whole intake is documented in [tax-intake.md](tax-intake.md). A client can still be created by hand and the deposit PaymentIntent pasted in, which is the path every pre-2026-09-17 plan took. Holistic Tax Priorities (program_id=1) clients don't see this phase — they arrive via MAP1.

**As of 2026-07-27 the Set Up phase contains exactly ONE task.** The former `tax_greenlight` ("Tax Plan Greenlight", Go/Stop) task is **deleted**, and the former "Refund Paid" task has been renamed and **relocated into Tax 1 - Diagnostic** as a single go/no-go decision — see [Step 0b](#step-0b--tax-plan-greenred-light-decision-inside-tax-1---diagnostic) below. **It now sits at `task_order=8` of Tax 1, with "Client risk profile complete" (9) the only step after it.**

| Task | status_options | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| Deposit Paid | `tax_deposit_pi` | **Portal-driven when an intake row carries the plan:** `utils/tax-intake-finalize.ts` copies the intake's `stripe_payment_intent_id` to `client_tax_plans.deposit_payment_intent_id` and writes this step `Completed` (the task is found by its sentinel, not its id), and the step shows a green **Paid via portal** chip with the deposit's `INV-`/`REC-` numbers riding on the same `tax_intake_load` payload. **Manual paste kept:** the text input is still there — admin pastes the Stripe PaymentIntent ID (`pi_...`) or a Stripe dashboard URL containing it, and `tax_save_deposit_pi` extracts the last `pi_...` substring (defensive against paste-over), writes it to `deposit_payment_intent_id`, and marks the task `Completed`. **On a waived intake** (two qualifying clients — see [tax-intake.md](tax-intake.md#the-deposit-and-the-waiver)) no PaymentIntent exists and finalize writes this row as **`N/A — No Deposit`** (2026-09-18), which the step renders as a chip with no `pi_` box and which every downstream gate accepts as done; the plan's stop route is then **Stop tax planning** rather than Refund (see [Step 0b](#step-0b--tax-plan-greenred-light-decision-inside-tax-1---diagnostic)), and the $250 leg is stamped `N/A — No Deposit` at plan creation. |

**Handler:** [`tax_save_deposit_pi`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/save-deposit-pi.ts) — AUTH (`ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` since 2026-09-17; it was in no gate list before).

---

## Step 0b — Tax Plan Red Light decision (inside Tax 1 - Diagnostic)

<a id="step-0b--tax-plan-greenred-light-decision-inside-tax-1---diagnostic"></a>

**Task:** "Tax Plan Green/Red Light - Refund $500 Deposit if unable to proceed based on the information provided" (`program_client_tasks` id **116**, sentinel `status_options='tax_refund'`, `phase_id=26`, **`task_order=8`**). **The row and its sentinel are NOT renamed** (#293 — both are lookup keys across two repos); only what the row PRINTS changed on 2026-09-21: `TASK_DISPLAY_LABELS` now renders it **"Tax Plan Red Light"** with the sub-label *"Refund the deposit, or stop tax planning, if unable to proceed"*. It sits immediately below **"Tax planner review complete"** (177, order 7) and is followed only by "Client risk profile complete" (117, order 9).

### What the step IS, since 2026-09-21 (Jake's decision)

**It is the STOP route and nothing else.** It appears **only** when "Tax planner review complete" reads **`Stop tax planning`** — or, for history, when it already carries a status (a legacy `Proceed`, the new `Stopped`) or a succeeded refund, in which case it renders as a done chip exactly as before. **On a Proceed it is not shown and not counted, on EVERY plan, deposit or not.** The old green **Proceed** button is **gone**: there is no decision left to make on this step once the reviewer has said carry on.

One predicate decides it in all three places (#339 keeps them in step):

| Where | Predicate |
|---|---|
| `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` (track view) | `redLightVisible = reviewStop \|\| depositRefunded \|\| isTaskStatused(redLightTask)` — drives `renderTask` (returns `null` when false) and `isStepExcluded` (drops it from the phase pill and the hero count) |
| `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` (`getPlanState`, the plan list) | the same three terms, read off that plan's own progress map |
| `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts` (Client Overview / planner portal) | `applicable: reviewStop \|\| redLightDone`, `done: status==='Proceed' \|\| status==='Stopped' \|\| depositRefunded` |

**Which button appears depends on the deposit, not on the program:**

| Plan | Button | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| Took a deposit (`deposit_payment_intent_id` set, or a Deposit Paid row that is NOT `N/A — No Deposit`) | **Refund** (red) | **Byte-identical to what shipped before** — the inline email-preview card, the required reason `<textarea>`, **Send Refund** to `automation_TAX_depositrefund`. |
| Waived intake (Deposit Paid row = `N/A — No Deposit`) | **Stop tax planning** (red) | The SAME card, with the new email's copy previewed in it, **Send** to `automation_TAX_stopnodeposit`. **No money moves** — there is no $500 to hand back. |

Both buttons open the same card component: the real subject line, `Hi [Client First],` (plus, on the stop route, the fixed opening sentence), the required reason `<textarea>` mid-body, the fixed closing paragraphs, and **Cancel** + **Send**. Send is disabled until a reason is typed, and `confirm()`s before firing.

### Refund (deposit plans) — unchanged

**Send Refund** fires `automation_TAX_depositrefund` with a **REQUIRED `reason`** body field (**400 `"reason required"`** without it), which: (1) fetches the PaymentIntent from Stripe to get the amount, (2) POSTs to `/v1/refunds` with `payment_intent=<saved pi_>` (full amount) under `Idempotency-Key: deposit-refund-tax-<plan.id>`, (3) writes `deposit_refund_id`/`deposit_refund_amount`/`deposit_refund_date`/`deposit_refund_status='succeeded'` (+ `status='stopped'`), (4) drafts the Gmail confirmation **to whoever paid the deposit**: template 181 `TAX_deposit_refund` **to the MEMBER** (the client's address is the fallback when the member has none, logged) or, when the plan's newest `tax_intake_requests` row says `payer='client'`, template 279 `TAX_deposit_refund|client` to the CLIENT with the member in Cc; a plan with no intake row at all (a hand-pasted deposit) takes 181. Subject `VFO Services - Tax Planning Deposit Refunded - [Client Name]`; the typed reason is substituted into the **`[Refund Reason]`** token (HTML-escaped, newlines to `<br>`, every token injected by function replacement so a `$` in the reason or the amount stays literal), (5) inserts the `TAX_deposit_refund_issued` FYI to the PF resolved through the client row (`taxPfRecipientsFor` — the MEMBER on a Direct plan, member-portal link, `forceRecipients`), and (6) **clears Tray's action-required `Tax Planner review complete for%` bell** — the refund IS that bell's instruction — **and the `Confirm the ROI meeting happened for%` ask** (`clearRoiConfirmBells`), which a closed plan can never answer. The idempotency key is `deposit-refund-tax-<plan.id>`, **rotated with a `-r<epoch>` suffix from `deposit_refund_failed_at` after a recorded failure** (#489; [tax-intake.md](tax-intake.md#the-refund--tax-plan-red-light-refund)). Refund columns are otherwise unchanged.

### Stop tax planning (no-deposit plans) — NEW 2026-09-21

**Send** fires [`automation_TAX_stopnodeposit`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/stop-no-deposit.ts) (AUTH, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, `denyIfNotPlannerPlan` like its sibling, deliberately **not** on `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`) with a **REQUIRED `reason`** (**400 `"reason required"`** without it). It:

1. **Refuses a deposit plan** — 400 `"This plan took a deposit; use Refund."` when `deposit_payment_intent_id` is set **or** the Deposit Paid row is not `N/A — No Deposit`. Two independent proofs, either of which refuses: a hand-created plan awaiting an admin's paste still owes the deposit and must not be stopped this way.
2. **Skips** with `{ok:true, skipped:true}` when `tax_stopped_at` is already set.
3. Writes `client_tax_plans.status='stopped'`, `tax_stop_reason`, `tax_stopped_at`, and the Red Light step's own progress row as **`'Stopped'`** with today's `completed_date` (the refund route proves itself through `deposit_refund_status` and writes no progress row — #293 — but this route has no such column, so the step row IS the record).
4. Drafts the Gmail to the CLIENT from **`email_templates` `TAX_stop_no_deposit`** — subject `VFO Services - Tax Planning Update - [Client Name]`, To `[CLIENT]`, Cc `[MEMBER, ASSIGNED_PF, Tracy, Tray]`, Bcc `[Anton, Paul]`, Draft mode; tokens `[Client First]` `[Client Name]` `[Member First]` `[Stop Reason]`, the reason HTML-escaped with newlines turned into `<br>` and every token injected by **function replacement** (#438), `gmailDraftFetch` keyed on the same `(pipeline, template_name)` pair that was SELECTed (#356), sandboxed by the client's TAX config, member additional contacts Cc'd exactly as the refund does. Stamps `tax_stop_email_sent` on success. **Fail-soft: a mail failure never un-stops the plan** (the PF bell says so in its message).
5. **Clears Tray's `Tax Planner review complete for%` bell** — stopping IS that bell's instruction on a no-deposit plan — **and the `Confirm the ROI meeting happened for%` ask** (`clearRoiConfirmBells`), unperformable on a closed plan.
6. Inserts the dismissible FYI **`TAX_plan_stopped_no_deposit`** (rule sort 270) to the PF resolved through the client row (`taxPfRecipientsFor` — the MEMBER on a Direct plan, `forceRecipients`, member-portal link), title `Tax planning stopped — <Client Name> (<client_ref>)`.

**Direct route:** the member may click either button on their OWN Direct plan — `tax_direct_stop_no_deposit` (beside the existing `tax_direct_deposit_refund`) via `directMemberHandler` + `TAX_DIRECT_MEMBER_ACTIONS`. On Direct the review bell that asked for the click and the FYI that reports it are the member's own (decision 28); Tray's copies exist only on classic plans.

### The $250 Tax Planning Team share moved to the REVIEW step (2026-09-21)

The leg used to ride this step's Proceed. With that button gone, **the trigger is now "Tax planner review complete" being saved `Proceed with tax planning`** (`actions/tax/save-task.ts`, in its own try/catch, program 4 only, only on a genuine status change), through the single shared entry point `fireDepositTeamShareForPlan` in [`utils/tax-deposit-team-share.ts`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/utils/tax-deposit-team-share.ts). **Every other property of the leg is unchanged** — its own status columns, the idempotency key and its rotation off a recorded failure (#489), the Connect capability probe before the key is spent, the `FAILURE_tax_deposit_team_share` bell, the terminal list (`Yes` / `N/A — No Deposit` / `N/A — Legacy`), and the `N/A — No Deposit` stamp written at creation on a waived intake. Its two retry/release sites moved with it: `tax_allocate_planner` and the nightly sweep now gate on `getReviewStepStatus === REVIEW_PROCEED` instead of `getGreenRedStatus === 'Proceed'` (that helper is deleted). The admin-only `$250 team share` chip now renders beside the **review step's** row, which is where it fires.

### The review bells, after 5d

| Plan | Review saved | Bell wording (Tray) | Cleared by |
|---|---|---|---|
| Any | `Proceed with tax planning` | "Set the client risk profile, then book the ROI meeting for the client." (the message only; the TITLE `Tax Planner review complete for <Client Name>` is the clear key and is untouched) | the ROI meeting being booked (`ready-for-tax3.ts`) or skipped (`skip-roi-meeting.ts`) |
| Program 4, deposit | `Stop tax planning` | "Click Refund on the Tax Plan Red Light step for \<Client Name\>." | the refund succeeding (`deposit-refund.ts`) |
| Program 4, no deposit | `Stop tax planning` | "Click Stop tax planning on the Tax Plan Red Light step for \<Client Name\>." | the stop being recorded (`stop-no-deposit.ts`) |
| Program 1 | `Stop tax planning` | "Complete the \"ROI Meeting booked - Send Confirmation Email\" step using the No button and enter the reason we are stopping." | the decline being recorded (`ready-for-tax3.ts`) |

`reviewBellNeedsGreenRed` is **deleted** — no Proceed path consults the Red Light step any more, so `ready-for-tax3.ts` and `skip-roi-meeting.ts` clear the Proceed bell unconditionally on every plan. The already-satisfied guard (an action-required bell whose instruction is already carried out is never minted, because no clear site would run again) is now `roiSettled` for Proceed and `deposit_refund_status==='succeeded' \|\| tax_stopped_at` / `ready_for_tax3_decision==='No'` for Stop. Booking or skipping the meeting over a Stop recommendation still overrides it and clears every Stop variant.

**Done-math (both repos):** done = progress `status === 'Proceed'` (history) **OR** `status === 'Stopped'` **OR** `client_tax_plans.deposit_refund_status === 'succeeded'`. `'Proceed'` and `'Stopped'` are exact-matched load-bearing strings in the FE `isTaskStatused` helper and in `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts`. The closed ladder gained a `tax_stopped_at` arm, **"Stopped — tax planning declined"**, beside the refunded one. See gotcha **#293**.

**Visibility:** when visible, the step is listed on **every** surface. The member view is read-only (the outcome chip only: a red `Refunded $500.00`, a red `Stopped`, or a legacy `Proceed`) **except on a Direct plan**, where the member owns the button. The **tax-planner portal shows it visible but LOCKED** — the task name is deliberately absent from both `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` whitelists (#262).

**Handlers:** [`automation_TAX_depositrefund`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/deposit-refund.ts) and [`automation_TAX_stopnodeposit`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/stop-no-deposit.ts) — both AUTH `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, both absent from `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`, both twinned for the Direct member (`tax_direct_deposit_refund` / `tax_direct_stop_no_deposit`). The $250 leg fires from `tax_save_task` on the **review** step's Proceed, through [`utils/tax-deposit-team-share.ts`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/utils/tax-deposit-team-share.ts) `fireDepositTeamShareForPlan`.

**Tables read:** `client_tax_plans`, `clients`, `members`, `tax_intake_requests` (the `payer`), `tax_planners`, `tax_planning_groups`, `pipeline_sandbox_config`(pipeline=`TAX`), `email_templates`.
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans`(deposit_payment_intent_id, deposit_refund_*, deposit_refund_email_sent, **tax_stop_reason / tax_stopped_at / tax_stop_email_sent**, status, deposit_team_share_status / _transfer_id / _at), `client_tax_progress` (Deposit Paid status + the Red Light step's `'Stopped'`), `notifications`.
**External calls:** Stripe `GET /v1/payment_intents/<id>`, Stripe `POST /v1/refunds`, Stripe `POST /v1/transfers` (+ the Connect capability probe and the destination-payment memo stamp), Gmail drafts API.

> **A Proceed on the review step is the normal lifecycle path** — this step never appears and flow continues to Step 1 below. Refund closes the engagement (`overview-tax.ts` reports `"Stopped — deposit refunded"`); Stop tax planning closes it as `"Stopped — tax planning declined"`. The whole deposit / Set Up / Red Light flow is **program_id=4 (standalone VFO Tax Planning) only**.

---

## Tax 1 - Diagnostic step order

The phase reads in the order the work actually happens: the client's returns are requested first (they are what start everything), somebody is allocated to them second, the information is gathered, the planner records their review call, and only then do the go/no-go and risk steps land. **Both programs share the order** except for the two steps program 1 does not have.

| Program 4 (VFO Tax Planning) — `phase_id=26` | | Program 1 (Holistic Tax Priorities) — `phase_id=18` | |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 172 Request Tax Returns (`tax_returns_request`) | 1 | 175 Request Tax Returns (`tax_returns_request`) |
| 2 | 118 Allocate Team Member / Tax Planner (`tax_planner_select`) | 2 | 84 Allocate Team Member / Tax Planner (`tax_planner_select`) |
| 3 | 119 Additional information required | 3 | 85 Additional information required |
| 4 | 120 Email to obtain information required sent *(hidden legacy)* | 4 | 86 Email to obtain information required sent *(hidden legacy)* |
| 5 | 121 Information received *(hidden legacy)* | 5 | 87 Information received *(hidden legacy)* |
| 6 | 122 Information passed to VFO-L *(hidden legacy)* | 6 | 88 Information passed to VFO-L *(hidden legacy)* |
| 7 | **177 Tax planner review complete** | 7 | **176 Tax planner review complete** |
| 8 | **116 Tax Plan Red Light** (`tax_refund`) † | 8 | 83 Client risk profile complete |
| 9 | 117 Client risk profile complete | | |

† **Display name only** — the stored row name and its `tax_refund` sentinel are unchanged (#293). **Shown only on a plan the reviewer STOPPED**: on every other plan, deposit or not, it is dropped from every surface and every count. See [Step 0b](#step-0b--tax-plan-greenred-light-decision-inside-tax-1---diagnostic).

The hidden legacy rows (86–88 / 120–122) stay filtered out of every surface — see gotcha #271. Tasks 84/118 are matched by the FE **sentinel (`tax_planner_select`) first**, with the pre-2026-08-10 name "Allocate to Advanced Tax Planner" kept as a fallback. Team Members are the deliberate FIRST holder of a plan and, since 2026-08-26, hold it in **their own slot** — see [Allocation is TWO slots](#allocation-is-two-slots-2026-08-26-v793) and gotcha #294 as amended twice.

**Migration note (2026-08-11).** The order above and the new step were applied by `20260811160000_tax1_reorder_planner_review_step.sql` (reorder + the new step at 7) and `20260811180000_tax1_request_first_and_chain_rules.sql` (Request Tax Returns to 1, Allocate to 2), both **DML only, applied live via MCP `apply_migration` and committed as files**. **No task ids changed, so ZERO `client_tax_progress` selections moved** — only `task_order` values, and there is no unique index on `(phase_id, task_order)`, so the intermediate collisions during the rewrite are harmless. One ledger discrepancy is recorded in the first file's own header: it was applied while the Stop option still read `'Stop tax planning - suggest refund'`, then renamed in-session by a follow-up live `UPDATE`, so `supabase_migrations` holds the pre-rename text while the committed file carries the final wording.

**Accepted consequence, carried forward:** `computeTrack`'s skipped-step warnings are **positional**, so every reorder retroactively changes `next_action` and the warning scan on plans that already exist — including the new step, which no existing plan has answered. The user has reviewed and accepted this on each reorder rather than change the seeding (#261 auto-seeds the risk profile at plan creation, and that step now sits last). See gotcha **#339**.

**Portal sessions need a page reload to see the new order** — the task template is module-cached client-side.

---

## "Tax planner review complete" — the reviewer's Proceed/Stop call (2026-08-11)

`program_client_tasks` **176** (program 1, `phase_id=18`) / **177** (program 4, `phase_id=26`), name exactly **`Tax planner review complete`**, `task_type='dropdown'`, `task_order=7`, `status_options='Proceed with tax planning|Stop tax planning'`. **A plain dropdown — there is no sentinel**, so the FE renders it through the generic dropdown path; its two statuses colour green `#1b9254` (Proceed) and red `#e74c3c` (Stop) in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`'s status-colour map.

The step exists because the plan needed a recorded reviewer decision between "we have the client's information" and "we spend money on this plan". Since 2026-09-21 it carries both consequences directly: its **Proceed forwards the $250 Tax Planning Team share** of a program-4 deposit and unlocks the ROI booking, and its **Stop is the only thing that raises the Tax Plan Red Light step**. It is **on all three planner-editable surfaces** (#262): the FE `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` set, the backend `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` exported from `save-task.ts`, and — because it saves through the ordinary `tax_save_task` — the existing allowlist entry for that action.

**Its two option strings are load-bearing.** `utils/tax-review-bell.ts` exports them as `REVIEW_PROCEED` / `REVIEW_STOP` alongside the step name (`REVIEW_TASK_NAME`), and every clear site in the hand-off chain decides what to do by reading this step's **stored status** rather than by parsing bell rows. Renaming the step or either option breaks that read. Answering it is also what fires Tray's outcome bell and (on Proceed) the planner's assess bell — see [the tax-track hand-off chain](#the-tax-track-hand-off-chain).

---

## Request Tax Returns — the same step in BOTH programs (2026-08-07)

Until 2026-08-07 only **VFO Tax Planning (program 4)** had a "Request Tax Returns" step (`program_client_tasks` **172**, sentinel `tax_returns_request`, first in Tax 1 - Diagnostic). **Holistic had no step at all** — its clients were asked for returns by an "Upload Tax Documents" button inside the **MAP 1 first-payment invoice/receipt email**, which minted `clients.tax_upload_token` and linked `/tax-upload`. That worked as an email and failed as a process: no request stamp, no received stamp, no step to look at, and no phase-scoped bell — the upload landed as a generic `UPLOAD_tax_return_uploaded` FYI and the tax track never moved. Holistic now has the real step (**task 175**), the first-payment email has given the job up ([contract-and-payment.md](contract-and-payment.md) Step 12, gotcha **#341**), and the whole model is gotcha **#340**.

**Handler.** `automation_TAX_request_returns` (`actions/tax/request-returns.ts`, AUTH, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` — deliberately **not** planner-allowed, unlike the additional-info twin, #262). Unchanged in shape: mints `clients.tax_upload_token` if absent, drafts a Gmail to the client carrying the secure `/tax-upload?token=` link and an "Upload Tax Returns" button, stamps `client_tax_plans.tax_returns_requested_at`.

**Per-program wording.** The plan's `program_id` is now in the handler's `.select()`, and the template is chosen `(plan.program_id || 1) === 1 ? 'TAX_request_returns|holistic' : 'TAX_request_returns'`, **falling back to the base name** when the variant row is missing or inactive. `email_templates` **214** (`TAX_request_returns|holistic`, pipeline `TAX`, **Draft mode**, To `[CLIENT]`, Cc `[ASSIGNED_PF, MEMBER, tnmiller@vfo-services.com, tvaldes@vfo-services.com]`, Bcc `[platham@elitert.com, aanderson@elitert.com]`) is a copy of row **194** differing only in its opening line ("We're now ready to begin work on the tax planning priority within your VFO Services Membership."), with #326-correct addresses. **`gmailDraftFetch` receives the template name ACTUALLY used** — that argument resolves the per-template Draft/Send toggle, so passing the hardcoded base name while drafting from 214 would silently apply the wrong row's send mode. The frontend mirrors the same ternary in the step's `StepEmailsChip` (`TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`) and `templateMeta.js` carries the matching description.

**Upload → received, and why the Holistic half is REQUEST-GATED.** `actions/vault/upload-notify.ts` runs **two independent lookups**: the newest **program-4** plan (unconditional — that step's request is implicit) and the newest **Holistic** plan, selected `.or("program_id.is.null,program_id.eq.1")` — `.eq` would exclude the NULL legacy rows — **and only when `tax_returns_requested_at` is set**. Every unstamped candidate gets `tax_returns_received_at`; the **action-required** `TAX_returns_allocate_team_member` bell (Tracy / Tray, title `Allocate a team member for <Client>`) fires **exactly once** — and is **skipped when every plan this upload stamped already has a planner**, because that instruction is already carried out and its clear site would never run again; a client holding plans in BOTH programs has both stamped by one upload. The request-gate is the compatibility layer, not a nicety: **`clients.tax_upload_token` is durable and per-client**, so every `/tax-upload` link sent by an older first-payment email is the SAME URL the new step mints — without the gate those old links would retro-green a step for Holistic clients who were never asked. An upload with no requested plan still falls through to the generic FYI, byte-identically to before.

> **v710 exists because the first shape was wrong.** v709 made the Holistic lookup an `else` on the program-4 one, so a client holding plans in both programs had program 4 **swallow** the upload and the Holistic plan never went green. Caught in click-testing, fixed by making the lookups independent and the stamp a loop. On a two-program client, "the newest plan" is never one query.

**"Already have tax returns" — the second button on the step (NEW 2026-08-16, v745).** Some clients' returns are already on file when the step is reached, so asking for them is noise. A second light-blue button beside "Send email" fires [`automation_TAX_returns_already_have`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/returns-already-have.ts) (AUTH, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, dispatched beside `automation_TAX_request_returns`) — **one click, no confirm dialog**. It stamps **only** `client_tax_plans.tax_returns_received_at`: **no email, no Gmail draft, no token minted, and `tax_returns_requested_at` stays NULL**. It is idempotent (an already-received plan returns `{success:true}` unchanged, so the date on the step never moves), and it re-uses `denyIfNotPlannerPlan` like every other plan-scoped tax handler (#257).

**It fires the SAME allocation bell as an upload, on the same condition:** when the plan has no `tax_planner_id`, the action-required `TAX_returns_allocate_team_member` bell goes to Tracy + Tray under the identical title prefix `Allocate a team member for <Client>` — only the message differs (*"…are already on file, please allocate a team member."*). That matters because the clear site (`allocate-planner.ts`, `clearAllocateTeamMemberBells`) matches on the **title prefix**, so the two paths must not diverge there (#365). The bell is fail-soft: the stamp is what the caller is waiting on.

**The stamp is PER-PLAN — a deliberate divergence from the upload path.** `vault/upload-notify.ts` stamps every unstamped candidate plan the client holds, so one upload can green both programs; this button stamps only the plan whose tab is open, so a client in both programs is clicked once per program tab. That is the intended behaviour (the admin is asserting a fact about the plan in front of them, not about the client).

**Display.** The done pill reads **"Returns on file"** when the step was never requested (`tax_returns_requested_at` NULL) and the date shows in the date column; a plan that WAS requested and then received keeps the existing "Returns received — \<date\>" pill.

**Done-state.** Like its program-4 twin the step is **column-proven and writes NO `client_tax_progress` row** — `done = !!tax_returns_received_at`, `at = received ?? requested`. `overview-tax.ts`'s `tax_returns_request` sentinel branch (added v708, gotcha **#339**) is therefore what keeps Client Overview honest for BOTH programs.

**Accepted consequence (explicit user decision).** Inserting the step at `task_order=1` means every existing Holistic plan's Tax 1 reopens as not-done until returns are requested and received, and the **12 Holistic plans already past Tax 1** gained a permanent "Request Tax Returns — not recorded" warning in Client Overview (#339's positional-warning mechanics). The user was shown this and accepted it. **(2026-08-18: those warnings are still COMPUTED and still shipped in the payload, but Client Overview no longer RENDERS them — the whole warnings UI was removed in the redesign, so the accepted consequence is now invisible rather than resolved.)** Portal sessions need a **page reload** to pick up the new step.

---

## Step 1 — ROI Meeting booked - Send Confirmation Email *(renamed + moved 2026-08-10, v717 — gotcha #358)*

**Trigger:** Admin clicks the **`ROI Meeting booked - Send Confirmation Email`** task in the Tax 2 phase ([TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx](src/components/admin/tax/TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx)). The task is `program_client_tasks` **90** (program 1) / **124** (program 4), sentinel `status_options='tax_3_decision'`.

> **What changed on 2026-08-10.** The task was **renamed** from "Tax 3 Confirmation Email" (itself a rename of "Ready for Tax 3?") and **moved to `task_order=0`**, so it is now the FIRST step of `Tax 2 - Deeper Dive` — the same `task_order=0` convention the Tax 4 confirmation step uses. Both changes are DML (`20260810130100_tax_roi_step_renames.sql`). Per gotcha **#339** the reorder is positional and retroactive: plans that passed this step un-completed now show a skipped-step warning on it, reviewed and accepted exactly as the 2026-08-07 Tax 1 reorder was. The FE matches the step by **sentinel first**, which is what made the rename safe to apply while the old bundle was still live.

**TWO buttons on Holistic, ONE on VFO Tax Planning.** **Send email (with date)** (green) opens the date / time / timezone form, with Send **disabled until a date is entered** and a Cancel beside it. **`No - Declined email to client`** (red) opens the inline red-dashed `[DECLINE_REASON]` textarea and renders where **this** step is the plan's stop route (`canDecline = (plan.program_id || 1) === 1 || noDeposit` in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`): Holistic, and a waived (no-deposit) program-4 plan. A program-4 plan that took a deposit stops through the Tax Plan Red Light **Refund** instead, so offering both would be two ways to close the same plan. *(A waived program-4 plan can therefore close two ways — this decline, or the Red Light "Stop tax planning". Its Stop bell names the Red Light button since 2026-09-21; the decline is left in place as the pre-existing route and clears the same bell.)* **That gate is FRONTEND-ONLY: the backend still accepts `decision:"declined"` on a program-4 plan** — a guardrail, not a boundary, the same shape as gotcha #353. **"Send email – date not confirmed" is GONE** — `decision:"confirm_no_date"` now returns **400 `"A meeting date is required"`**, deliberately kept as its own named branch so an old cached bundle gets a legible refusal rather than a generic one.

**Handler:** [`automation_TAX_readyfortax3`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/ready-for-tax3.ts) — AUTH handler.

**What it does:**
1. Validates `client_tax_plans` row exists (created earlier by `tax_start_plan`). (No longer hard-blocks on `ready_for_tax3_email_sent='Yes'` — re-sends are allowed.) **A plan with `roi_meeting_skipped_at` set is refused outright — 400 `"The ROI meeting has been skipped for this plan"`, on BOTH decisions including the decline** (skipping is final; see [Step 1b](#step-1b--skip-the-roi-meeting-both-programs-2026-08-16-v746)). `confirm_no_date` → **400**; `confirm_date` → **400 unless `meeting_date` matches `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$`**; **any non-`declined` decision → 400 `Complete the "Client risk profile complete" step before booking the ROI meeting` unless that Tax 1 step's plan-level row carries a `Risk 1–5` grade** (2026-09-21, 5g — see the gate section above). **A confirm send still 400s unless the plan has a `tax_planner_id` allocated**, now worded **"Allocate a Team Member / Tax Planner before sending the confirmation email"** because either role may hold the plan (#294 as amended); the `declined` path is exempt. The Yes template gets `[Planner Name]` substitution + the `TAX_PLANNER` Cc chip.
2. Loads `clients`, `members`, `pipeline_sandbox_config` (pipeline='TAX').
3. UPDATEs plan: `ready_for_tax3_decision`=`Yes` (confirm) / `No` (declined), `ready_for_tax3_email_sent='No'` initially, `sandbox`.
4. Loads `email_templates` row — `confirm_date` → `TAX_readyfortax3|Yes`; `declined` → `TAX_readyfortax3|No`.
5. Substitutes `[Client Name]`, `[Client First]`, `[Member Name]`, `[PF Name]`, plus **`[CLOSING]`** ("on \<date\> at \<time\> \<tz\>") on the Yes template and `[DECLINE_REASON]` on the No template.
6. Creates Gmail draft to client (CC member + PF; BCC `aanderson@elitert.com` + `platham@elitert.com`).
7. Flips `ready_for_tax3_email_sent='Yes'` **and, on `confirm_date`, PERSISTS `tax3_meeting_date` / `tax3_meeting_time` / `tax3_meeting_timezone`.** Those three columns are the only source for the step's **"Meeting booked for M/D/YYYY at h:mm AM TZ"** pill, for the nightly sweep's assess-reminder window and for that email's `[Meeting Date]`/`[Meeting Time]`/`[Meeting Timezone]` tokens. The decline path never writes them. **Legacy rows survive display-only:** plans confirmed before v717 have no trio, so the pill falls back to the stored progress status and the old `'Yes - Confirmation email (date TBC)'` value is deliberately retained in the FE colour map.
   **RESCHEDULE (NEW 2026-08-16, v747).** The booked pill now carries a **Reschedule** button that reopens the same date/time/timezone form pre-filled from the stored trio and re-sends the SAME `TAX_readyfortax3|Yes` template. **When the date actually changes and *either* assess-reminder stamp was already set, the handler NULLs BOTH `tax3_assess_reminder_sent_at` and `tax3_assess_reminder_early_sent_at`** (the early tier, 2026-08-20) so both tiers of the planner's before-meeting assess reminder re-arm against the new date — that ladder counts *backwards from the meeting* (#359), so unlike every other reschedule in this family it SHOULD re-arm (#404). The condition is an OR and the clear is unconditional on both columns: a plan that only ever got the early nudge still re-arms, and a plan pushed far enough out can legitimately receive the early tier a second time. The sweep's own guards (form submitted / vault drop / date in range) still apply, so a plan whose assess form has since been submitted gets nothing.
8. **Hand-off chain, fail-soft (gotchas #357/#179).** All of it is wrapped in one try/catch — a bell can never fail an email that has already been drafted. In order: (a) either decision clears this client's unread action-required bells titled `Book the ROI meeting for%` — **that bell has no call site any more, but unread rows of that shape still exist in production, so the clear stays**; (b) the **review-outcome** bell (`Tax Planner review complete for%`) is consumed here per the matrix in `utils/tax-review-bell.ts` — on `confirm_date` a recorded Proceed clears it **unconditionally on every plan** (since 2026-09-21 booking or skipping is the whole Proceed instruction; nothing pairs with it), a recorded Stop also clears because booking anyway overrides the recommendation, and on `declined` **every variant clears unconditionally**, since declining IS the Holistic Stop bell's instruction and makes any Proceed variant moot; (c) on `confirm_date` with a **team member holding the plan and NO Tax Planner allocated beside them** (`!tax_planner_id && tax_team_member_id` — since 2026-08-26 a plain column test, because the planner slot can no longer hold a Team Member, so the old `planner_role` lookup on this path is deleted) it fires `TAX_team_member_allocate_planner` — "open the client and allocate the Tax Planner who will run the plan".
