**Tables read:** `clients`, `members`, `pipeline_sandbox_config`, `email_templates`. *(The `tax_planners` `planner_role` read is GONE as of 2026-08-26 — the two slots make it redundant.)*
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans` (update), `notifications` (clear + insert).
**External calls:** Google OAuth, Gmail drafts API.
**Chains:** none.
**Read-only viewers** (member portal / planner `readOnly`) get a Not-started pill instead of the controls — **except a DIRECT member, who owns this step** (2026-09-18): the FE offers them **Send email (with date)** and **No - Declined** (plus Reschedule once booked; the two admin **Skip ROI** buttons are hidden in `directMode`) through `tax_direct_readyfortax3`, and the FE then writes the booked/declined status through `tax_direct_save_task` (the `tax_3_decision` sentinel is in `DIRECT_MEMBER_EDITABLE` for exactly that write). The 5g risk-profile 400 above applies to the twin exactly as to the admin action.

---

## Step 1b — Skip the ROI meeting: TWO routes (both programs, 2026-08-16 v746; second route 2026-08-17)

Some clients go **straight to the detailed tax plan meeting**: there is no ROI meeting, so the whole `Tax 2 - Deeper Dive` phase and Tax 3's `ROI Presentation` step never happen. **Skipping is FINAL** — there is no un-skip and no booking afterwards. Full trap inventory: gotchas **#405** (the done-math split) and **#407** (the two routes).

**What differs between the routes is ORDER, and nothing else.** Both stamp `roi_meeting_skipped_at`, so every mechanism keyed on *"was it skipped?"* — the skipped-away rows, Tax 2's green Done 1/1, the `ready-for-tax3.ts` refusal, the `|skipped` email axis, the `overview-tax.ts` mirror — is **route-blind** and was not touched when the second route shipped. Only four FE step gates and the bell sequence read the mode.

| | `retainer_first` (the original; also **NULL**) | `meeting_first` |
|---|---|---|
| Order | decision → sign → pay → detailed meeting | detailed meeting → confirm it happened → decision → sign → pay |
| Asked first, by the skip | **PF**: `Complete the Client tax planning decision for` | **Planner**: `Send the detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email for` |
| `Client tax planning decision` unlocks | at the skip | once `Detailed tax plan presentation` is answered |
| `Client decision 1` also waits on | — | the retainer being paid |

**Columns.** `roi_meeting_skipped_at timestamptz` (`20260816230000_tax_roi_skip_column.sql`) — the one-way "was it skipped" stamp, nothing ever clears it. `roi_skip_mode text` (`20260817100000_tax_roi_skip_mode_column.sql`) — the discriminator, **NULL reads as `retainer_first` everywhere**, so a plan skipped before the column existed keeps the behaviour it was created under. **No backfill was needed or performed:** at the time of writing no live plan had been skipped. **No CHECK constraint** — no column on this table has one; `skip-roi-meeting.ts` is the only writer and validates the value, so widening the set later is a deploy, not a migration.

**Buttons.** TWO light-green buttons on the `ROI Meeting booked - Send Confirmation Email` step, beside Send / Decline: **Skip ROI — retainer first** and **Skip ROI — meeting first**. Both keep their `confirm()`, whose text spells out the ordering, because the action *and the route* are irreversible.

**Handler:** [`automation_TAX_skiproimeeting`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/skip-roi-meeting.ts) — AUTH, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, `denyIfNotPlannerPlan`-guarded. One action for both routes; `mode` is the only difference.

**Guards, then the stamp:**
- `mode` absent/empty → `retainer_first`; any other unrecognised value → **400** listing the accepted set;
- already skipped **on the same mode** → `{success:true}`, **no re-stamp** (a repeat click must not move the date shown on the step);
- already skipped **on the OTHER mode** → **400** naming the route it actually took. Deliberately a refusal rather than a silent no-op: the two routes unlock different steps and have already asked different people for different things, so nobody may believe they changed it;
- `tax3_meeting_date` set **or** `ready_for_tax3_decision === 'Yes'` → **400 `"The ROI meeting is already booked for this plan"`**;
- no `tax_planner_id` → **400 `"Allocate a Team Member / Tax Planner before skipping the ROI meeting"`** (same ownership rule as the confirm send);
- then `roi_meeting_skipped_at = now()` **and** `roi_skip_mode = mode`.

**No email** on either route. Skipping is an internal routing decision, not something the client is told at this step.

**Bell hand-off — everything after the stamp is fail-soft in one try/catch** (a bell must never fail a skip that is already written). Shared by both routes: (a) clears legacy `Book the ROI meeting for%` rows; (b) runs the **review-outcome matrix exactly as booking does** — a recorded `Proceed` clears `Tax Planner review complete for%` **unconditionally on every plan** (2026-09-21: skipping settles the booking half, and that is the whole Proceed instruction now), and a recorded `Stop` also clears, because skipping and carrying on overrides the recommendation just as booking anyway does; (c) clears the planner's `Enter the tax planning opportunities for%` and the PF's `Download the ROI presentation for%` (`clearPresentationDownloadBells`) — both point at Tax 2. Then (d) **asks whoever owns the next step on the chosen route**, each mint carrying its own satisfied-on-fire guard (#365).

**The full bell sequence per route** — mapped in the `utils/tax-review-bell.ts` header, which is the source of truth:

| # | `retainer_first` | `meeting_first` |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `skip-roi-meeting.ts` → PF, `Complete the Client tax planning decision for` | `skip-roi-meeting.ts` → **Tray** (planner until 2026-08-18), `Send the detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email for` (**`TAX_roi_skipped_hlm_ready`** — key name is historic) |
| 2 | `invoice-receipt.ts` → **Tray** (planner until 2026-08-18), `Send the detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email for` (`TAX_planner_hlm_ready` — key name is historic) | `revshare-sweep.ts` → planner, `Complete the tax plan review steps for` — **the presentation ONLY** |
| 3 | `revshare-sweep.ts` → planner, `Complete the tax plan review steps for` — presentation **AND** Client decision 1 | `save-task.ts` → PF, `Complete the Client tax planning decision for`, once the presentation confirms the meeting happened |
| 4 | — | `invoice-receipt.ts` → planner, `Complete Client decision 1 for` (**`TAX_roi_skipped_decision1_ready`**) |

**Two rules added for the second route** (`20260817100100_…` sort 31, `20260817100200_…` sort 32; both `ALLOCATED_TAX_PLANNER`, `action_required`):
- **`TAX_roi_skipped_hlm_ready`** deliberately **shares its title** with `TAX_planner_hlm_ready`, so `highlevel-meeting-confirm.ts`'s existing `clearHlmConfirmBells` retires it with no change there. Two keys, one title: the key controls routing and Editor enable-ability, the **title** controls clearing — the same precedent as `Complete the tax planner review for`.
- **`TAX_roi_skipped_decision1_ready`** needed its **OWN** title, `Complete Client decision 1 for`. Reusing `Complete the tax plan review steps for` would have been fatal: on this route that prefix is satisfied by the presentation **alone**, so the second ask would have been cleared by the next clear-site run before anyone read it. **Two asks separated in time need two titles.** Cleared by `postreview-decision.ts` on **every** branch, `Stop - Refund` included. The `1` keeps it non-overlapping with `Complete Client decision 2 for` under `ILIKE`.

`TAX_roi_skipped_decision_needed` (the decision ask, `sort=222`, routed on the PF's **portal login** address via `taxPfLoginEmail` on a classic plan and on the **allocated Tax Planner + Team Member** via `notifyAllocatedPlanner` on a **Direct** plan since 2026-09-21 — phase 5f, because that step is the planner's on Direct; prefix exported as `ROI_SKIPPED_DECISION_PREFIX`) is now fired from **two** sites depending on the mode — `skip-roi-meeting.ts` on `retainer_first`, `save-task.ts` on `meeting_first`. Titles are identical on both routes and both recipients, so the single clear site retires either vintage (#411). Cleared **only** by [`decision.ts`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/decision.ts), on **any** decision (Yes / Undecided / No).

**Backend refusals behind the FE locks (#403 — a lock is choreography, never a boundary):**
- `ready-for-tax3.ts` **400s BOTH decisions** on a skipped plan, either route — that one-way-ness is what makes the rest safe.
- `decision.ts` 400s on a `meeting_first` plan whose `Detailed tax plan presentation` is unanswered.
- `postreview-decision.ts` 400s on a `meeting_first` plan whose `retainer_invoice_email_sent !== true`. The **idempotent already-decided return stays FIRST**, so a double-submit that in fact succeeded can never fall into this gate and 400.
- `save-task.ts`: the review-Proceed bell's `roiBooked` became **`roiSettled`** (booked **OR** skipped) in the `alreadySatisfied` guard — which, since 2026-09-21, is the guard in full.
- `overview-tax.ts`: the four Tax 2 steps **and** a newly-named `ROI Presentation` branch carry `applicable: reach && !roiSkipped` (the **#339** mirror). On `meeting_first` it additionally **reorders** the Tax 4 meeting steps — the confirmation email, the synthetic *Generate detailed tax plan presentation* (2026-09-25) and *Detailed tax plan presentation*, three in all — ahead of the Tax 3 decision and widens their `applicable` from `yesReach` to `afterSetup` — `computeTrack` is strictly positional, so without the move the whole pre-payment window reports the wrong `next_action` and then raises a false skipped-step warning. The move is a no-op if the list is already ordered.
- `confirmation-email.ts` and `revshare-sweep.ts` carry **route-dependent wording** — see #406.

**Display rules — the split is the part that is easy to get wrong (#405).**
- **`tax_3_decision` counts as DONE when skipped** (`isTaskStatused: … || roiSkipped`), because "there will be no meeting" is a real answer to it. That is what makes **Tax 2 render a green Done 1/1** on the phase card and in the hero rather than an empty phase.
- The other four (`assess_form`, `tax_generate_presentation`, `tax_presentation_link`, `ROI Presentation`) are **excluded from the counts** and render grey **`Skipped`** rows — but **only when unanswered**: a step actioned before the skip keeps its normal row and stays counted, because that work really happened (`isSkippedAway` tests `!isTaskStatused(t)`).
- The booking row renders a **green dot + green chip naming the ROUTE** — `ROI meeting skipped — retainer first` / `— meeting first` — with `roi_meeting_skipped_at` in the date column. The route is on the chip because the two orderings are otherwise indistinguishable on that row, and which one a plan took decides what everyone downstream is waiting for.
- Skipped rows render on **every** surface — admin, member (`readOnly`) and planner — so the history reads identically to everyone. Skip outranks the lock gate, and no hint is shown (there is nothing to wait for).

**Tables read:** `client_tax_plans`, `client_tax_progress` (the review step's status), `clients`, `notification_rules`.
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans` (`roi_meeting_skipped_at`, `roi_skip_mode`), `notifications` (four clears + one insert).
**External calls:** none. **Chains:** none.

---

## Step 1¼ — Generate and download presentation (Tax 2 - Deeper Dive, added 2026-08-06 v706; TWO-YEAR v2 rewrite 2026-08-07 v713; four-total inputs + always-single-year 2026-08-17; **TWO-YEAR MODE RESTORED — decided by the form, as of 2026-08-18 (v759, template v4)**; **template v9 2026-09-24 — three logos on every slide + an Additional Benefits slide**; **template v10 2026-09-25 — TPOM wording, two new slides, a real Pay IRS figure, so 30 OR 32 slides**)

The ROI deck generator. It sits at **`task_order=2`** in **Tax 2 - Deeper Dive** (`program_client_tasks` ids **173** prog 1 Holistic / **174** prog 4 VFO Tax Planning, name "Generate and download presentation" — **displayed as "Generate TPOM Presentation"** since 2026-09-25 — sentinel `status_options='tax_generate_presentation'`) — inserting it pushed ids 90/124 to `task_order=3` and the "Send presentation link" step (168/169) to **4**. **(90/124 have since moved AGAIN — to `task_order=0` on 2026-08-10, so the ROI confirmation step now precedes everything in this phase; 173/174 and 168/169 were not renumbered, so the phase reads 90/124 → … → 173/174 → 168/169.)** It replaces a human assembling the deck by hand from numbers already stored on the plan.

**Handler:** [`tax_generate_presentation`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/generate-presentation.ts) — **AUTH, `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, deliberately NOT planner-callable** (the FE renderer is also hidden when `readOnly || plannerMode`). Full invariants: gotcha **#336**. The logo resolver, the risk-grade read + arrow placement and the Drive upload live in **`utils/roi-deck-branding.ts`** (moved verbatim on 2026-09-25 so the Tax 4 book-ends generator uses the same definitions — see Step 12¾).

**Readiness gates — the FE spells each one out rather than greying the button silently (rewritten 2026-08-17; year-2 gate added 2026-08-18):**
- assess form not submitted → *"Submit the tax planning opportunities form first"*
- submitted but **not in the flat year-1 shape** — an old strategy-format row, or one missing any of `fee` / `taxes_without_plan` / `taxes_with_plan` / `cash_outlay` → *"Re-save the Assess form — it uses the old strategy format"*; the handler's matching 400 is *"Re-save the Assess form — it uses the old strategy format; re-enter the four totals"*. **The generator is strict new-shape-only** — it derives nothing from the old per-strategy rows.
- **a `year2` group that is PRESENT but incomplete** (any of its three totals missing/negative, or `taxes_with_plan >= taxes_without_plan`) → *"Re-save the Assess form — the Year 2 totals are incomplete"*, both as the FE hint and as the handler's 400. **An ABSENT `year2` is not an error** — it is simply a one-year plan. The save handler only ever writes the key once all three validated, so a present-but-broken group means the row was hand-edited or written by something else; the generator therefore re-validates all three rather than trusting it.
- no risk grade → *"Set the Client risk profile step first"*
- `taxes_without_plan <= 0` → 400 *"Total Tax without the Plan must be greater than zero"* (it is a divisor)
- `taxes_with_plan < 0` or `>= taxes_without_plan` → 400 *"Total Tax with the Plan must be less than Total Tax without the Plan"* (same string the save path uses)

**What Generate does, end to end.** Reads the plan + `assess_form` + the **plan-level** risk grade (`client_tax_progress` where `tax_specialist_id IS NULL`, task "Client risk profile complete", parsed `/Risk\s*([1-5])/`) → downloads **`ROI-template-master-v10.pptx`** from the private **`presentation-templates`** bucket → **refuses a master missing any of `REQUIRED_TEMPLATE_PARTS`** (the two logo media parts + `slide30.xml`, `slide31.xml`, `slide32.xml` — an older master uploaded under the v10 name, loud 500) → **swaps the two shared logo parts' BYTES for the group and member logos (see "Logos" below; a logo problem is a warning, never a failure)** → **decides the FEE MODE and, in 2-payment mode, runs the fee-row surgery on slides 24 and 28 (see below)** → substitutes **31 `{{TOKENS}}`** across the **NINE** parts of `TOKEN_PARTS` — the eight slides **1/20/23/24/25/27/28/29** plus **`ppt/notesSlides/notesSlide23.xml`** (XML-escaped) → **writes the planner's assess-form notes into slide 23's SPEAKER notes (see below)** → **in single-year mode only, performs the slide-24 header surgery and drops BOTH year-appendix slides (see below); in two-year mode drops nothing** → repositions the slide-20 `upArrow` to the client's risk band off a hardcoded EMU table (1→801612, 2→2566499, 3→4349395, 4→6126289, 5→7900180) → re-zips with **fflate** (untouched entries **stored at level 0**, only the edited XMLs deflated — the PNG media, including the two swapped logo parts, is never recompressed; a generated deck is ~32.5 MB) → uploads to Google Drive **multipart with metadata `mimeType='application/vnd.google-apps.presentation'`**, which is what makes Drive convert it into a **real Google Slides file**, into an **"ROI Presentations"** folder under `GOOGLE_DRIVE_FOLDER_ID` → sets sharing **role=writer type=anyone** (deliberate, user-approved over view-only after a security review) → reads `webViewLink` → stamps `client_tax_plans.generated_presentation_{drive_id,url,at,by}` → returns `{success, url, generated_at, logos: {group, member}, logo_warnings}`. Runs ~30–60s, which is why `src/lib/api.js` grants this action **90s** via `LONG_TIMEOUT_ACTIONS` (writes still never retry, #30).

**THE DECK IS 30 OR 32 SLIDES, AND `assess_form.year2` IS THE ONLY THING THAT DECIDES (2026-08-18, #374; counts per template v10).** Between 2026-08-17 and 2026-08-18 it was *always* single-year; that is now the **single-year** branch of two, and the branch condition is the mere PRESENCE of the nested `year2` group — nothing else, no flag, no column.

- **`year2` ABSENT → single-year, 30 slides.** Both appendix slides are dropped AND slide 24's year header is deleted with the shapes below it shifted up — **nine of them on a 2-payment deck, ten on a 3-payment one** (v6 added a third fee row, and v8 merged every fee row's value shape into its label and deleted it, taking both counts down by four; see the fee-mode section below for why the count is mode-dependent). Byte-for-byte the same output the always-single-year build produced.
- **`year2` PRESENT → two-year, 32 slides.** Nothing is dropped, both appendix slides ship, and slide 24 keeps its header reading **"Your investment: 2026 + 2027"**.
- **⚠ THE SINGLE-YEAR LAYOUT IS NOW PRODUCED AT GENERATION TIME, NOT BAKED INTO THE MASTER.** This is an INVERSION of the 2026-08-17 arrangement. Template **v4 carries the header**, and `dropInvestYearsHeader()` in the handler reproduces the old static layout on the fly: it deletes the one `<p:sp>` containing `{{INVEST_YEARS}}` and shifts every remaining `<p:sp>` whose `<a:off>` has **`x < 4800000`** (left column only — the right "Net Tax Savings" panel starts at `x=4876920` and must not move) and **`y >= SHIFT_MIN_Y = 1625410`** (the blue bar's top on v9) up by **`HEADER_SHIFT_EMU = 539650`**. Exactly **1** deletion and exactly **`SHIFT_EXPECTED_2PAY = 9` or `SHIFT_EXPECTED_3PAY = 10`** shifts are asserted (they were 12 / 14 on v6 and v7; template v8 deleted four value shapes from that column — #480) — the count is **passed in by the caller from the FEE mode**, not derived here, so this assertion stays independent of the fee-row surgery's; either count being wrong is a loud 500, not a silently holed deck. It runs on the RAW xml **before** substitution, because the shape is located by the literal token substitution is about to erase. **These constants are COUPLED TO THE TEMPLATE VERSION** — the geometry ones were measured off v4's `slide24.xml` and byte-verified to reproduce the old live master's `slide24.xml`; v8 moved only the two shape COUNTS; **v9 moved slide 24's whole left column UP to clear the logo row** (header and blue bar together, so `HEADER_SHIFT_EMU` and both counts are unchanged and only `SHIFT_MIN_Y` follows the bar's top). A template rebuild MUST re-derive them; nothing else in the package pins them — `make_v9.py` prints the old-vs-new negative controls.
- **In two-year mode the drop block does not run at all** — it is guarded by `if (!twoYear)`, and that one condition covers both the appendix removals and the slide-24 surgery.
- **The appendix slides are `ppt/slides/slide28.xml` (year 1) and `slide29.xml` (year 2)** — in single-year mode they are removed, not blanked. Per slide, four things go together and missing any one makes PowerPoint call the file corrupt: the `<p:sldId>` in `ppt/presentation.xml`, the `<Relationship>` in `ppt/_rels/presentation.xml.rels`, the `<Override>` in `[Content_Types].xml`, and the part plus its `.rels` in the zip.
- **⚠ THE TWO REMOVALS SHARE THREE FILES.** `presentation.xml`, its rels and `[Content_Types].xml` are read out of the zip **once** and the progressively-patched strings are threaded through both loop iterations. Re-reading the originals for the second slide silently discards the first removal and ships a corrupt deck that passes every assertion on the way out.
- **The rId is resolved dynamically** per slide, by matching the relationship whose `Target="slides/slideN.xml"`, then finding the `<p:sldId>` carrying that `r:id`. It is never hardcoded. Each XML patch asserts **exactly one match** and returns a **loud 500** naming the part, the slide and the count if not.
- **`SLIDE_PARTS` lists 28 and 29 in BOTH modes** so their tokens are substituted whether or not the parts survive — that is what keeps the leftover-token guard uniform across all **9** parts. Do not remove them from the list, and do not make the list conditional. **Since template v5 the loop, the missing-part 500 and the guard all run over `TOKEN_PARTS` (= `SLIDE_PARTS` + `NOTES_PART`), not `SLIDE_PARTS`** — and the notes part is dropped in **neither** mode, because what single-year removes are the two appendix SLIDES and those have no notes part at all.
- **⚠ FILENAMES ARE NOT POSITIONS.** `sldIdLst` order is `1–21, 31, 22, 23, 24, 28, 29, 32, 25, 26, 30, 27` (template v10): **Tailored Tax Strategies (`slide31.xml`)** displays at 22, the year appendices `slide28.xml` / `slide29.xml` at **26 and 27**, **Tax Strategy Toolkit (`slide32.xml`)** at 28, then "Choose Who You Pay" / Double-Guarantee / **Additional Benefits (`slide30.xml`)** / Congratulations at **29/30/31/32** (single-year: the two appendices go, so the Toolkit is 26 and Congratulations 30). Parts are addressed by filename; never "correct" `SLIDE_PARTS` to display numbers.
- **Consequence for anyone reading a generated deck:** the slide count is **not fixed** — read `year2` on the plan before assuming either shape, and never assume fixed display positions past display 24. This is the generated OUTPUT only — the template in the bucket always has all 32.

**THE FEE MODE — A SECOND, INDEPENDENT AXIS (2026-08-27, template v6 / v795). The deck now mirrors the revised tax fee process; see [flows/tax-fee-process.md](tax-fee-process.md), which is the authority on the money.** The template used to hard-code ONE fee shape: a *"One-Time Fee"* with two 50% rows under it, both filled from the single `{{FEE_HALF}}` token. Since 2026-08-25 an engagement has **two** shapes, so v6 stops naming the rows and starts tokenising them.

- **The branch:** `const shape = splitFeeCents(feeCents)` — the shape is **IMPORTED, not re-derived** *(2026-09-01, 2nd; it used to be `feeCents > THREE_PAYMENT_THRESHOLD * 100` with the arithmetic written out locally)*. There are **THREE** bands now, separated by two constants: `THREE_PAYMENT_THRESHOLD = 30000` and `THREE_PAYMENT_MIN = 31000`. **STRICT at both edges** — exactly $30,000 is a plain 2-payment split, and **exactly $31,000 is 3 payments** (final retainer $500, the smallest that exists).
- **⚠ `splitFeeCents` is IMPORTED from `constants/tax-fee-process.ts`, never re-typed.** It is the **same function `deriveFeeSplit()` uses to price the engagement**, so the deck and the money can never disagree about where 2 payments become 3, or about what the buffer band pays. **A change to the fee process changes the deck automatically — and any future change to the bands or the flat retainer must assume the deck follows it.**
- **The rows** (slides 24 and 28 carry the SAME three rows with the SAME values — the year-1 appendix mirrors the ROI slide deliberately, because the fee is a single engagement-level figure attributed entirely to year 1):

  | assess fee | mode | row 1 | row 2 | row 3 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | `≤ $30,000.00` | 2-payment | Retainer Payment: `round(fee/2)` | Implementation Fee: the remainder | *(shapes deleted)* |
  | `$30,000.01 – $30,999.99` — the **BUFFER BAND** *(2026-09-01)* | 2-payment | Retainer Payment: **$15,000 flat** | Implementation Fee: `fee − 15,000` | *(shapes deleted)* |
  | `≥ $31,000.00` | 3-payment | Initial Retainer Payment: **$15,000 flat** | Final Retainer Payment: `round(fee/2) − 15,000` | Implementation Fee: the remainder |

  A buffer-band deck is a 2-payment deck in **every** respect — row 3 is deleted and chip 2 reads *"Retainer Payment non-refundable"* exactly as any other 2-payment deck does. The band exists because a 3-payment split just above $30,000 produced silly final retainers ($30,119 → $59.50); see [flows/tax-fee-process.md → The buffer band](tax-fee-process.md#the-buffer-band).

- **The chips** (slide 27's three Congratulations chips) carry the same figures: `CHIP1_LABEL`/`CHIP1_VALUE` mirror row 1, `CHIP3_VALUE` is the implementation payment, and **`CHIP2_TEXT` is the ONE token whose value carries its own `$`** — every other money site has the dollar sign as template text, but chip 2 has an amount in 3-payment mode (*"Final Retainer Payment: $8,019 (non-refundable)"*) and none in 2-payment mode (*"Retainer Payment non-refundable"*; the "Retainer Payment" / "Implementation Fee" names date from template v10, 2026-09-25), so no fixed literal works.
- **2-payment surgery — `dropFeeRow3(xml, part, shiftBack)`.** Deletes row 3 from **both** fee slides, located by its literal tokens, on the RAW xml before substitution. **Since template v8 that is ONE shape carrying BOTH `{{FEE_ROW3_*}}` tokens, not two shapes carrying one each** — the handler asserts exactly 1 shape and exactly 1 of each token, so a v7 master uploaded under a v8 name fails here rather than shipping half a row (#480). `shiftBack` is **slide 24 ONLY**: v6 pushed slide 24's *"Your investment in VFO Tax Specialists:"* header (shape 1528, widened to `cx=5155530` with `wrap="none"` at v8 so the longer v7 wording cannot wrap onto the row below) and its *"Plan Investment: ${{CASH_OUTLAY}}"* row (shape 1529, whose text lives in an `<a:t xml:space="preserve">` run a naive `<a:t>` scan misses) DOWN 317400 EMU to make room, so with the row gone they go back up by the same amount, restoring v5's exact geometry. **SLIDE 28 WAS NEVER SHIFTED** — its rows sit higher and row 3 fit with 165000 EMU of clearance — so it passes `shiftBack=false` and **must keep doing so**.
- **⚠ THE TWO SURGERIES CAN BOTH LAND ON SLIDE 24, AND THE ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING. The fee-row surgery runs FIRST.** Its un-shift selects shapes by an ABSOLUTE y threshold (`FEE_BELOW_MIN_Y = 3700000` on v9 — it sits between row 3 and shape 1528, both moved up by v9), and the header surgery would already have moved those shapes 539650 EMU above it — run the other way round, the threshold silently matches nothing and the count assert fires for the wrong reason. Because the row surgery changes how many shapes sit below the header, `dropInvestYearsHeader()` is **told** how many to expect (9 or 10). **Each surgery asserts on its own evidence; composing two surgeries on one part is only safe if neither can mask the other's failure.**
- **⚠ THEN A THIRD, GEOMETRIC CHECK — `assertClearsLogoRow` (template v9).** Once both surgeries have run, no `<p:sp>` on slide 24 or slide 28 may end below **`LOGO_ROW_TOP_EMU = 4680000`**, the top of the bottom logo row; any that does is a loud 500 asking whether an older master was uploaded under the v9 name. It exists because shape COUNTS cannot tell a v8 slide 24 from a v9 one (v9 moved a whole set of shapes together, so every count survives) — v8 fails this check in both two-year modes. Checked in whichever of the four modes the deck is.
- **Cent-exact, and taken from `splitFeeCents()` rather than `deriveFeeSplit()`** — the latter THROWS outside $0–$60,000, and a legacy plan whose assess fee sits outside that band must still generate a deck. `splitFeeCents()` carries **no range check for exactly this reason**. The sum identity (`initial + final + implementation === fee`) is still asserted in code and 500s if it fails.
- **⚠ THE DECK ALWAYS QUOTES THE FLAT $15,000 — BY USER DECISION.** The 2026-08-26 custom-initial-retainer allowlist (`CUSTOM_SPLIT_CLIENT_IDS`) is deliberately **NOT** wired in: an allowlisted client's deck shows the standard figure. The deck predates the custom quote, the custom split moves only the initial/final boundary *inside* the retainer half, and the client sees the real figures on the agreement and the invoice.
- **⚠ THE ASSESS FEE IS THE ONLY SIGNAL, AND IT HAS TO BE.** This deck is generated on *"Tax 2 - Deeper Dive"*, **upstream of every fee-process write**: `fee_process_version`, `initial_retainer_amount` and `final_retainer_amount` are all stamped at the Tax 3 decision. At generation time there is no row to read and `isThreePaymentPlan()` would answer false on every plan. **Do not "improve" this into a column read.**
- **⚠ NOTHING VALIDATES THAT THE FEE IS LESS THAN THE GROSS BENEFIT.** A fee large enough to exceed the savings produces a legitimately NEGATIVE *"Net Tax Savings"* on a client-facing slide, rendered honestly in accounting parentheses. There is no guard on the assess form. See the OPEN/OWED entry in `SESSION_REFERENCE.md`.
- **Package-wide, the string *"50%"* is now GONE** — from every slide AND every speaker-notes part. `make_v6.py` asserts a count of zero across all 570 entries; `NOTES_FIFTY_SURVIVORS` is an empty named dict so a future deliberate exception has a home.

**Values on the deck (per-year as of 2026-08-18; the derivation is still INVERTED at the top).** Year 1 comes from the flat totals **`W1`=`taxes_without_plan`, `T1`=`taxes_with_plan`, `C1`=`cash_outlay`** and the engagement-level **`F`=`fee`**; year 2, when present, from `year2.{taxes_without_plan, taxes_with_plan, cash_outlay}` = **`W2`/`T2`/`C2`**. `taxes_with` is **ENTERED** and `gross = W − T` is **DERIVED** — the exact inverse of the old model, where the gross was summed from strategies and `taxes_with` fell out of it.

- Per-year: `GROSS_Y1 = W1 − T1`, `GROSS_Y2 = W2 − T2` (0 when single-year), `INVEST_Y1 = C1`, `INVEST_Y2 = C2`.
- **FEE ATTRIBUTION — the whole fee lands on YEAR 1 and none on year 2:** `NET_Y1 = GROSS_Y1 − INVEST_Y1 − F`, `NET_Y2 = GROSS_Y2 − INVEST_Y2`. That is the deck's original rule and the reason **slide 29's fee value is a literal `$0` in the template, not a token** — there is no `PLANNING_FEE_Y2`, and `make_v4.py` asserts one has not crept back in. **v6 renamed the LABEL above it from *"One-Time Fee:"* to *"Total Fee:"* on all three fee surfaces (24, 28, 29) and changed nothing else about either value** — slide 29 gets the label and nothing else, by user decision, so the three surfaces agree. **v10 renamed it again to *"Total Plan Creation Fee:"*, the header above it to *"VFO Tax Plan Creation Fee:"*, and *"VFO Tax Specialists"* to *"VFO Tax Strategies"*, on the same three surfaces.**
- **Every combined figure is the SUM of the per-year figures**, computed from them specifically so the two can never drift: `TAXES_WITHOUT = W1+W2`, `TAXES_WITH = T1+T2`, `GROSS_BENEFIT = GROSS_Y1+GROSS_Y2`, `CASH_OUTLAY = INVEST_Y1+INVEST_Y2`, `NET_BENEFIT = NET_Y1+NET_Y2`, `OUTLAY_PLUS_FEE = CASH_OUTLAY + F`, `PCT_REDUCED = round(GROSS_BENEFIT/(W1+W2)×100)`. **`FEE_HALF` is RETIRED as of template v6** — the fee breakdown is now the six `FEE_ROW*` tokens plus the four chip tokens, derived by the fee mode above.
- **The three identities that hold by construction** and are the checks to run against any generated deck: `NET_Y1 + NET_Y2 === NET_BENEFIT`, `GROSS_Y1 + GROSS_Y2 === GROSS_BENEFIT`, `INVEST_Y1 + INVEST_Y2 === CASH_OUTLAY`.
- `year` = the **generation-time** calendar year, `year2 = year+1`; **`INVEST_YEARS` is `"<year> + <year2>"` in two-year mode and `String(year)` in single-year mode** — where the shape carrying it is deleted, so that value goes nowhere (harmless: substitution is split/join and the leftover guard only fires on tokens left UNreplaced).
- In single-year mode the six appendix tokens still substitute — `GROSS_Y1`/`INVEST_Y1`/`NET_Y1` carry the whole-plan figures and the `_Y2` trio are `$0` — kept only so all 30 slide-borne tokens resolve before their slides are dropped. **The row-3 tokens are substituted in 2-payment mode too, for the same reason** — their shapes are already deleted, so the values go nowhere, and the leftover-token guard stays uniform.
- Money is whole-dollar with commas — **the cents-tolerant tokens are the fee-breakdown ones** (`FEE_ROW{1,2,3}_VALUE`, `CHIP1_VALUE`, `CHIP2_TEXT`, `CHIP3_VALUE`), since half of an odd total legitimately carries cents; negatives use accounting parentheses, and `NET_BENEFIT` (and `NET_Y1`, which carries the whole fee) can legitimately come out negative. **Cosmetic, pre-existing, first seen 2026-08-27:** a negative renders as `$(21,038)` rather than the conventional `($21,038)`, because the `$` is template text OUTSIDE the token. Only ever visible on a negative net.

**PLANNER NOTES GO INTO THE DECK'S SPEAKER NOTES (2026-08-19, template v5 / v761).** The optional **Notes** textarea on the assess form (`assess_form.notes`) is rendered onto **slide 23's speaker notes**, below the closing script line and a blank spacer, as **`TAX PLANNER NOTES: <what the planner typed>`**. It is presenter-only — nothing appears on any slide face — and it is the reason the deck carries **`{{PLANNER_NOTES}}`, the first token ever to live outside `ppt/slides/`** — the 22nd on template v5, and **the 31st on v6** (#336). **`notesSlide23.xml` is still the ONLY tokenised notes part**: v6 edited `notesSlide26` and `notesSlide27` too, but only to de-percent the two speaker scripts, so neither joined `TOKEN_PARTS`.

- **Multiline notes survive.** A bare `\n` inside `<a:t>` renders as nothing in PowerPoint and Slides, so the handler rewrites the token's whole `<a:r>` run into **one run per line joined by `<a:br>`**, cloning the run's own `<a:rPr>` onto every rebuilt run and break so the styling matches the line above it. This is why `PLANNER_NOTES` is **not** in the generic value map: its replacement is XML, and the generic loop escapes its values.
- **No notes → no label.** An empty or absent `notes` produces one empty invisible run; the paragraph stays and renders as nothing, so a deck for a plan with no notes shows no dangling *"TAX PLANNER NOTES:"* heading. This is the ordinary path, not a branch.
- **The run is found by index walk, never by regex** — see **#420** for why the obvious pattern silently ate 7 KB of speaker notes in simulation.
- **Both deck modes carry it.** The notes part is never dropped.

**LOGOS ON EVERY SLIDE + THE ADDITIONAL BENEFITS SLIDE (2026-09-24, template v9).** Every slide carries three logos, all **`<p:pic>`** — never `<p:sp>`, because every slide surgery above selects `<p:sp>` blocks and a picture is invisible to them: the **tax planning group bottom-LEFT, the member bottom-MIDDLE, VFO bottom-RIGHT** (the blue wordmark `image27.png`; slides 4/5/6 gained it), all **395000 EMU tall on a row whose top is y=4680000**. The **title slide** instead carries one even row across the TOP — group / member / VFO (the white wordmark), 560000 EMU tall, y 300000–860000. Slides 1–6, 14 and 24 were re-laid-out in the master to clear the row; the handler touches none of that.

- **The media contract.** Every group picture points at ONE shared part, **`ppt/media/logo_group.png`**, and every member picture at **`ppt/media/logo_member.png`**. In the master both are a fully transparent 1200×400 PNG — a blank slot. The handler replaces those two parts' **BYTES** (stored, level 0) and never edits slide XML to place a logo, so one swap fills every slide.
- **Resolution.** **Member** = the plan's client → `clients.member_number` → `members.logo_image`, used **only when `members.logo_enabled === true`** (the member's *"Show my logo on ROI presentations"* switch). **Group** = `plan.tax_planner_id ?? plan.tax_team_member_id` → `tax_planners.member_type` (the group NAME) → `tax_planning_groups.logo_image`. Files come from the public **`headshots`** bucket via the service role and are re-checked with the same validator the uploads use (`utils/logo-png.ts validateLogoPngBytes` — PNG, IHDR exactly 1200×400, ≤ 1 MB), because the frame is 3:1 and anything else would stretch.
- **⚠ EVERY LOGO PROBLEM IS A WARNING, NEVER A FAILED DECK.** A download error, a file that fails validation, a missing member / planner / group row, a planner with no group, a client with no member number or a plan with no planner allocated leaves that slot blank and adds a line to **`logo_warnings`** in the response (also `console.warn`ed). A logo that is switched off or simply absent is a CHOICE and leaves the slot blank **silently**. A group with no logo uploaded (Dorado Strategies at ship) shows a blank group slot until one is.
- **The white badge is baked into the logo PNG**, not drawn by the deck: `src/components/shared/logoPng.js` (`fileToLogoPng`) trims the source's near-white/transparent margins and contain-fits it into a white rounded badge inside the 1200×400 canvas, so a logo reads on the blue title slide and the white content slides alike. The deck cannot draw a badge shape itself — it would be a `<p:sp>` on surgery slides. Logos are uploaded by `member_branding_save` (member / admin) and `tax_planning_group_logo_save` (admin) — see [tables/members.md](../tables/members.md) and [tables/tax.md](../tables/tax.md).
- **The Additional Benefits slide** is part **`ppt/slides/slide30.xml`**, displayed right after the Double Guarantee and before Congratulations (display **31** of 32, **29** of 30), on EVERY deck — token-free and never dropped. It keeps the *"$2600"* line the agreement's Additional Benefits section leaves out.
- **Known, pre-existing:** the slide-6 quote image reads *"for the Second Circui"* (missing `t`) — it is in the image itself and needs a corrected image, not code.

**TEMPLATE v10 — THE TPOM WORDING PASS (2026-09-25, v892; built by `scripts/roi-presentation/make_v10.py` from the live v9 plus the business's TPOM Google Slides deck).** Only the words Jake listed and the two new slides were taken from that source — its formatting is not authoritative, and its older wording elsewhere ("TAX Proactive Planning" on slides 7–12, the Double-Guarantee "50%", the slide 1 / 27 speaker notes) was deliberately NOT applied.

- **Wording:** title *"Tax Planning / Orientation: / ROI Meeting"* (three lines, box moved up to the source's y); Step 3 on slides 7–13 *"3. Orientation"* with the description *"Outline VFO Tax Process & High Level ROI"* (10–13); notesSlide10's heading *"Tax Step 3: Orientation"*; notesSlide22 (the slide-23 script) = the source's, *"[Innovation / SDP / Restrepo Wealth]"*; the fee-surface labels above. **Slide 24's geometry is byte-identical to v9, so no surgery constant moved** — including the empty band under *Plan Investment* on a single-year 2-payment deck, which v9 created and Jake chose to keep (2026-09-25).
- **Pay IRS with a plan is REAL.** "Choose Who You Pay" (part `slide25.xml`, shape 1561) was the literal `$0`; it now carries `${{TAXES_WITH}}`, so each row sums to the no-plan total: **`TAXES_WITHOUT = OUTLAY_PLUS_FEE + TAXES_WITH + NET_BENEFIT`**. The only token change in v10 — one more SITE of an existing key, so the value map is unchanged. That slide's speaker script still says *"eliminate the IRS payment shown here"* (the business's own copy, left as-is).
- **Two new token-free slides**, never dropped: `slide31.xml` *Tailored Tax Strategies* and `slide32.xml` *Tax Strategy Toolkit*, each with its speaker notes (`notesSlide28` / `notesSlide29`) and the three logos, on a copied empty layout (`slideLayout177.xml`, master 5). **The Toolkit's icons are glyphs of the *Material Icons* font** — they render in Google Slides, where every generated deck is opened, but show as words (*"movie"*, *"home"*) in a PowerPoint without that font, so a local render is NOT evidence against them (#541).
- **Live-proven** on Test Client 62 plan 216 (Jake, 2026-09-25): a 2-payment single-year deck — the new slides, the icons, the new labels, and Pay IRS `$100,000` beside `$9,000` + `$41,000` = `$150,000`. The 3-payment and two-year shapes were proven by a local PowerPoint render of a simulated fill only.

**Buttons:** Generate → then **Open** (opens the Slides URL — there is no local file; it also fires `tax_presentation_downloaded`, which retires the PF's *"Download the ROI presentation for…"* bell) and **Regenerate** (creates a **NEW** Drive file each time; the plan row points at the newest, old files are not deleted).

**The step is done when `generated_presentation_at` is non-NULL** — the handler writes **NO** `client_progress` / `client_tax_progress` row, and both `actions/clients/overview-tax.ts` and the FE `isTaskStatused` read the column directly.

**Deliberately NOT auto-filled:** the Tax 2 "Send presentation link" field below. An admin reviews the generated deck and pastes the link manually, so `member_presentation_link` and `presentation_link` are untouched by this step.

**Template maintenance cycle** (the newest step is **`make_v10.py`**, which reads the pinned live v9 + the business's source deck; see `scripts/roi-presentation/README.md` in the edge repo) — edit a copy in Google Slides → download as .pptx → **rebuild** through the script chain `build_master.py` → clone step → `edit_v2.py` → `revise_v2.py` → **`restyle_23.py` → `restyle_bullets.py`** → **`make_v3.py`** (applies the two v3 edits — the slide-24 title/`INVEST_YEARS` swap and the slide-26 bullet removal — to an unpacked v2 package, every edit expect-asserted) → **`make_v4.py`** (verify-and-rename only: it asserts the base's SHA256, 570 entries / 29 slides / 27 notes parts / 21 tokens, `{{INVEST_YEARS}}` present, `{{PLANNING_FEE}}` present and **neither** `{{PLANNING_FEE_Y1}}` nor `{{PLANNING_FEE_Y2}}` anywhere, then `shutil.copyfile`s it) → **`make_v5.py`** (the slides 7–13 retitle to *"VFO Tax Planning Process"* — matched on the whole two-run PAIR, never the bare word `TAX`, which also appears in *"6 TAX PLANNING STEPS"* on every one of those slides — plus the `{{PLANNER_NOTES}}` paragraph appended to `notesSlide23.xml`, whose part name is PROVEN from `ppt/slides/_rels/slide23.xml.rels` rather than assumed) → **`make_v6.py`** (the REVISED TAX FEE PROCESS: seven edits and nothing else — *"One-Time Fee:"* → *"Total Fee:"* on slides 24/28/29 with the three-run `One|-|Time` label collapsed to one run keeping run 1's `rPr`; the two 50% rows on 24/28 tokenised and a THIRD row cloned one 317400 EMU pitch lower; slide 24's below-section pushed down to match; slide 27's three chips tokenised with the literal `"50% "` prefixes gone — **chip 3's was a whole PARAGRAPH, deleted whole**, which is why chip 3's text now sits ~5.3pt higher than chip 1's first line; slide 26's guarantee de-percented; and `notesSlide26`/`notesSlide27`'s speaker scripts de-percented — *"the initial 50% deposit"* → *"the initial deposit"* (a deletion) and *"invoice of 50% tax planning retainer"* → *"invoice of THE tax planning retainer"* (a **REPLACEMENT**, because a bare deletion is ungrammatical and a presenter reads the line aloud). It asserts a **package-wide `"50%"` count of ZERO across all 570 entries**, slides and notes alike) → **`make_v7.py`** (2026-09-09, from the live v6: ONE wording edit, *"Your investment in VFO Specialists:"* → *"Your investment in VFO Tax Specialists:"* on slides 24/28/29, one `<a:t>` run each, with the sibling *"Your investment in VFO Services:"* asserted UNTOUCHED three times) → **`make_v8.py`** (2026-09-09, from v7: shape 1528 `cx 4363200 → 5155530` EMU with `wrap="none"` + `<a:noAutofit/>` so the longer header cannot wrap, and each fee row's VALUE shape merged into its LABEL shape and deleted — 1527→1526, 1531→1530, 1533→1532, 1536→1535 — asserting an IDENTICAL token multiset, 570 entries / 29 slides / 27 notes, and printing the surviving slide-24 shape ids the handler's shift constants are measured from) → **`make_v9.py`** (2026-09-24, from the live v8: the three `<p:pic>` logos on every slide wired to the two shared `logo_group.png` / `logo_member.png` parts, the new `slide30.xml` Additional Benefits slide, the layout nudges on slides 1–6/14/24, and slide 6's quote image — which it needs from Jake's local file `Judge J. Learned Hand.png`, sha256-pinned and NOT in the repo; it asserts the token multiset identical to v8's, 30 slides with slide30 at display 29, and prints the re-derived constants plus the old-constant negative controls) → re-upload as exactly `ROI-template-master-v9.pptx`. **`make_v5.py` reproduces STAGE 1 of v5 ONLY** — the live v5 object is that output plus the two 2026-08-19 same-name hot swaps (the slide-23 notes trim and the slide-3 agenda retitle), which must be re-applied by hand from their dated backups after any rebuild; **`make_v6.py` takes the LIVE v5 object as its base** (`BASE_SHA256 = e479ad2a…`) and so already includes them. **On a genuine rebuild the baked `BASE_SHA256` in `make_v4.py`/`make_v5.py`/`make_v6.py` will not match, which is correct and expected** — re-derive it, and re-derive **both** sets of handler constants with it: the four slide-24 header-shift ones (`SHIFT_EXPECTED_2PAY` / `SHIFT_EXPECTED_3PAY` are **9 / 10**, `SHIFT_MIN_Y` **1625410** on v9) **and** v6's three fee-row ones (`FEE_ROW_SHIFT_EMU` 317400, `FEE_BELOW_MIN_Y` **3700000** on v9, `FEE_BELOW_EXPECTED` 2) **and** v9's `LOGO_ROW_TOP_EMU` (4680000). **Full detail lives in `scripts/roi-presentation/README.md` in the EDGE repo** — read it before touching the deck. The master is always rebuilt, never hand-patched (every re-export shifts the paragraph/run indices the scripts edit by), and **the restyle steps are not optional**: a fresh export carries the old styling, so skipping them silently reverts the look while the numbers stay perfectly correct.

**How a deck change ships — two paths, and the choice is not yours to make freely (#336/#342).** A change confined to **token-free** slides (styling, layout, colour, bullets) ships by **replacing the SAME bucket object: no code, no deploy**, live on the next generation — that is exactly how the 2026-08-07 slide-2/3/4/28 restyles went out after v713. A change to **any token, the slide count, a part filename or `sldIdLst`** requires a **NEW versioned object name plus a lockstep backend deploy** (upload first, keep the superseded object as rollback) — that is why the two-year rewrite became `…-v2.pptx` and why `ROI-template-master.pptx` still sits in the bucket. **The 2026-08-12 v3 change took the second path** — it added a token (`INVEST_YEARS`) and made the slide count variable — so it shipped as a NEW object `ROI-template-master-v3.pptx`, uploaded BEFORE the v729 deploy, with v2 left in place as the rollback. **The 2026-08-17 four-total / always-single-year change took NEITHER path: `TEMPLATE_OBJECT` stayed at v3 and no master was re-uploaded**, because no token was added, renamed or dropped — only the values behind them and which parts survive into the output. **The 2026-08-18 two-year restoration took the SECOND path again, for a reason worth understanding: v4 has NO content delta at all.** It is **byte-identical** to the dated backup `ROI-template-master-v3-pre-shift-2026-08-17.pptx` (SHA256 `319593587028fdad36429e3349819ddb132707462676a3722d8cfc54e8d1385b`). What made it a new version is that the *live* v3 object had been hot-swapped on 2026-08-17 to DELETE slide 24's `{{INVEST_YEARS}}` header, so restoring that header is — **relative to the object the live function actually reads** — a token-SITE **addition**. That is the versioned path by the rule, regardless of the bytes being an old friend. Uploaded BEFORE the v759 deploy; v3 stays as the rollback. **The 2026-08-19 change took BOTH paths, in sequence, on the same day — and that is the clearest worked example the bucket holds.** Stage 1 was **versioned**: retitling slides 7–13 would have been a hot swap on its own (seven token-free slides), but the same build added `{{PLANNER_NOTES}}` to `notesSlide23.xml` — a token site in a **wholly new part** — so it shipped as `ROI-template-master-v5.pptx`, uploaded before the v761 deploy, with v4 retained. Stages 2 and 3 were then **hot swaps** on that same object: trimming slide 23's speaker-note label list (backup `…-v5-pre-trim-2026-08-19.pptx`) and retitling slide 3's agenda item (backup `…-v5-pre-agenda-2026-08-19.pptx`), both rename-first. **Stage 2 is the subtle one — it edited a part that NOW CARRIES A TOKEN and was still a legal hot swap, because it left the token's own `<a:r>` run byte-intact.** The test is not "is this part in `TOKEN_PARTS`?" but *"does this edit move, duplicate, restyle or delete the token's run?"* — and because that is far finer than a filename, prove it by diffing the run out of both packages rather than eyeballing it (#336). **The 2026-08-27 v6 change is the least ambiguous versioned case the bucket holds: it both ADDS nine token sites and RETIRES one (`{{FEE_HALF}}`), across five slides and two notes parts.** A hot swap was never on the table. Uploaded BEFORE the v795 deploy, v5 retained as the rollback. **The 2026-09-09 pair — v7 then v8 — took the versioned path TWICE IN ONE DAY, and v7 is the case that shows a "pure wording" edit is not automatically a hot swap.** v7 changed seven characters of one header on slides 24/28/29 and moved no token, but those three slides CARRY tokens, so the hot-swap rule does not reach them; it shipped as `ROI-template-master-v7.pptx` before the v820 deploy. **It then rendered wrong** — the longer header wrapped out of its fixed-width box and overlapped the *"Plan Investment:"* row (#480) — so v8 widened shape 1528, forbade wrapping and merged every fee row's value shape into its label, shipping before the v821 deploy with **v6 and v7 both retained as rollbacks**. v8 changed no token and no slide count, but it deleted shapes the handler counts, which is the other half of the versioned test: **an edit that moves the numbers the CODE asserts needs a lockstep deploy exactly as much as one that moves a token.** **The 2026-09-24 v9 took BOTH paths again.** It added parts the handler requires (the two logo media parts and `slide30.xml`), changed the slide count and `sldIdLst`, and moved the slide-24 shapes two thresholds measure, so it shipped as `ROI-template-master-v9.pptx` before the lockstep deploy, **v8 retained as the rollback**. Jake's same-day review of a real deck then produced a **hot swap of the same v9 object** (slide 2's Disclaimer box shortened, slide 3's agenda lines raised, slide 6's new quote image) — legal because it touched only non-surgery, token-free slides and no part the handler names.

**Tables read:** `client_tax_plans`, `client_tax_progress`, `clients`, `program_client_tasks`, `members` (`logo_image`, `logo_enabled`), `tax_planners` (`member_type`), `tax_planning_groups` (`logo_image`). **Storage read:** `presentation-templates` (the master), `headshots` (the two logos).
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans` (`generated_presentation_drive_id`, `_url`, `_at`, `_by`).
**Chains:** none. **No emails, no notifications.**

---

## Step 1½ — Send presentation link to member before meeting (Tax 2 - Deeper Dive)

A scheduled, cron-drafted step that sits after "Generate and download presentation" in the **Tax 2 - Deeper Dive** phase (`program_client_tasks` ids 168 prog 1 / 169 prog 4, `status_options='tax_presentation_link'`, **`task_order=4` as of 2026-08-06** — it was 3 until the generator step was inserted at 2). Lets the admin queue a presentation link to go to the **member** ahead of the Tax 3 ROI meeting, sent on a chosen date. **Not applicable on a DIRECT plan** (2026-09-18): the member IS the PF and already holds the deck, so `utils/tax-plan-steps.ts` marks the step `applicable=false` when `plan.tax_route === 'direct'` and `TaxPrioritiesTab` hides it, uncounted, with the gate that keys on it satisfied; the `tax_direct_presentation_schedule` twin is registered but no control reaches it.

**Schedule handler:** [`automation_TAX_presentation_schedule`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/presentation-schedule.ts) — AUTH. Admin clicks the green "Schedule email" button → pastes a link + picks a date → handler writes `member_presentation_link`, `presentation_send_date`, `presentation_scheduled_at` (and nulls `presentation_email_sent_at`). **No email here.** Uses its OWN `member_presentation_link` column — deliberately separate from the Tax 3 `presentation_link` (Step 2) so the two never overwrite each other.

**Sweep handler:** [`automation_TAX_presentation_sweep`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/presentation-sweep.ts) — PUBLIC service-role; daily cron `tax-presentation-sweep-daily` at **09:00 UTC**. Selects plans where `member_presentation_link IS NOT NULL` AND `presentation_email_sent_at IS NULL` AND `presentation_send_date <= today`; for each, drafts the `TAX_presentation_link` email (id 151) **To the member, Cc the assigned PF** (`[Member First]` greeting, `[PRESENTATION_LINK]` → "View the presentation" button linking the pasted URL).

> **The client's Additional Contacts ride ONLY on the client-fallback branch (2026-08-27, v798).** A member with no email on file makes the To fall back to `client.email`; only then is `loadAdditionalContacts` called at all. Until v798 it was called unconditionally, so a client's Cc contact was Cc'd on an email addressed to the member — observed live (Ally Buehrie on the client Pat Hurst's presentation draft). This **reverses** the 2026-08-20 decision that kept this sweep wired because "the client is the subject"; the rule is now that the Cc follows the **To**. See [additional-contacts.md](additional-contacts.md).

After each successful draft the sweep stamps `presentation_email_sent_at` and **clears the assigned PF's action-required `Download the ROI presentation for%` bell** (`clearPresentationDownloadBells`, fail-soft after the draft has already been created). **This sweep is the ONLY site that stamps `presentation_email_sent_at`** — `presentation-schedule.ts` merely NULLs the column when a new send is scheduled. **It is no longer the only site that CLEARS that bell, though (v726):** `presentation-schedule.ts` now calls `clearPresentationDownloadBells` too, the moment a send date is scheduled — scheduling *is* the PF's job done, and waiting for the 09:00 UTC draft left the bell in their inbox for up to a day after the work was finished. The sweep's clear stays as the backstop for bells raised on plans scheduled before v726. **Drafts only — no auto-send**, consistent with every other automation. The frontend step shows green "Schedule email" → blue "Scheduled — <date>" (with Edit) → green **"Email drafted for \<date\>"** (wording since 2026-08-20; it read "Email drafted — \<date\>" before). **The date is the SEND date either way** — `presentation_send_date`, the same value the Scheduled chip shows — not the date the draft was created; only the phrasing changed, to stop the dash reading as "drafted on".

**Tables read:** `client_tax_plans`, `clients`, `members`, `pipeline_sandbox_config`, `email_templates`.
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans` (`member_presentation_link`, `presentation_send_date`, `presentation_scheduled_at`, `presentation_email_sent_at`).
**Chains:** none.

---

## Step 2 — Tax 3 — "Client tax planning decision"

**Trigger:** Admin opens the `Client tax planning decision` task in Tax 3 phase → fills `TaxDecisionForm` ([TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx](src/components/admin/tax/TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx)) → submits. The form submits in 2 API calls back-to-back: `tax_save_task` (writes the progress row with status `Completed - <decision>`) then `automation_TAX_decision`.

**Handler:** [`automation_TAX_decision`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/decision.ts) — AUTH handler. Takes `tax_plan_id`, `decision` (Yes/Undecided/No), `form_data` (JSON). **Gate (2026-09-21, decision 29):** `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` **and** `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` Direct-only — `denyIfPlannerOnClassicPlan` runs directly after `denyIfNotPlannerPlan`, so on a **classic** plan only an admin records the decision (a planner 403s `VFO Services records this decision on this plan`) and on a **DIRECT** plan the **allocated Tax Planner** does, from the planner portal (the member is locked out — chip **Tax Team**, hint *Handled by the Tax Planning Team*; `tax_save_task` lets the planner write this one step's row on Direct via `DIRECT_PLANNER_TASK_NAME`). On Direct the Yes branch ignores the submitted split and writes **`split_type='Direct 45/45/10'`** — 45% member / 45% tax planning group / 10% VFO Services of `total_fee`, whole cents, VFOS taking the rounding residual (`directSplitColumns` in `pricing.ts`, the first of the three split writers; `TaxDecisionForm` still offers the 1/3 option on Direct — flagged, the server wins). Every decision also retires the two PF "record the decision" asks (`clearClientDecisionBells` + the ROI-skipped clear), and the chain to `automation_TAX_sendagreement` carries the CALLER's token — the planner's on Direct.

> **Member signing & paying on behalf** (added this session, mirrors MAP 1 PIP-Follow-Up). The `TaxDecisionForm` carries a Yes/No "is the member signing & paying on the client's behalf?" question. When Yes, `automation_TAX_decision` writes `client_tax_plans.member_paying_on_behalf=true` (default false), and the flag **carries through Tax 3 → Tax 4 → Tax 5**. When true, every downstream email flips **To member / Cc client**, uses the **member email-template variant** (template_name + the suffix ` (member signing/paying on clients behalf)` — 22 member rows, `email_templates` ids 126–147), the **member is the BoldSign signer 1 and the Stripe customer/payer**, and invoice/receipt **"Bill To" / "Received From" = member**, with a **"Client: <client name>"** line printed under the member (`onBehalfOfName`, 2026-09-23 — on the retainer, final-retainer, implementation and amended invoices/receipts). 14 tax handlers branch on the flag: decision, send-agreement, stripe-customer, payment-email, confirmation-email, invoice-receipt, paidbycheck, final-decision, extra-meeting, postreview-decision, refund, implement-decision, implementation-receipt, charge-implementation; plus `revshare-sweep.ts` `sendReminderEmailUnified` (all 5 reminder types flip to the member template). **NOT changed:** `ceo-countersign` (always Anton), `revshare` (pays the member their share regardless of who paid), and the Setup-phase `deposit-refund` (the deposit predates the Tax 3 decision; since 2026-09-17 its email is addressed by `tax_intake_requests.payer` instead — see [Step 0b](#step-0b--tax-plan-greenred-light-decision-inside-tax-1---diagnostic)).

> **Skip-aware template selection (NEW 2026-08-16, v748).** When the ROI meeting was skipped there was no meeting to thank anyone for and no presentation to re-read, so the decision emails have their own copy. [`utils/tax-decision-template.ts`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/utils/tax-decision-template.ts) `pickDecisionTemplate()` resolves **two independent axes → four names per step**:
>
> | | client signing/paying | member signing/paying |
> |---|---|---|
> | **normal** | `TAX_decision_undecided` | `TAX_decision_undecided (member signing/paying on clients behalf)` |
> | **skipped** | `TAX_decision_undecided\|skipped` | `TAX_decision_undecided\|skipped (member signing/paying on clients behalf)` |
>
> Same grid for `TAX_decision_decline` and `TAX_agreementsent|Yes`. **Six new rows, `email_templates` ids 220–225**, all seeded **Draft** by `20260816233000_tax_decision_skipped_template_variants.sql`. The copy edits are: the subject drops the word **"Meeting"**, meeting-attended phrasing becomes *"Thank you for taking the time to explore tax planning…"*, and the whole *"I also attach a link to your presentation … [PRESENTATION_LINK]"* sentence is **removed**. **Fallback rule:** a missing/inactive `|skipped` row falls back to the non-skipped name **keeping the member suffix** — a member-paying plan must never fall back onto the client-addressed copy. **`pickDecisionTemplate` returns the name that ACTUALLY resolved and that is the name handed to `gmailDraftFetch`**, because that argument resolves the per-template Draft/Send toggle (#207/#356). Wired into `decision.ts` (Undecided + No) and `send-agreement.ts` (Yes).
>
> **Typo fix shipped alongside** (`20260816233100_…`): row **20** (`TAX_agreementsent|Yes`, client-paying) read *"I also attach a link your presentation"* — now *"a link to your presentation"*. Idempotent, body only; the member twin (row 129) was already correct. It had to land on the normal-case row or it would never land at all, since the `|skipped` copy deletes that sentence entirely.

**What it does (branches by decision):**

> **Diron Insley display-only discount** (added 2026-07-14). For clients whose `clients.member_number` = `59073` (`constants/tax-discount.ts DISCOUNT_MEMBER_NUMBER`), all three pricing forms (`TaxDecisionForm`, `TaxPricingForm` for the deferred Undecided→Yes path, and the extra-meeting outcome form) show a "Discount applied due to previous Diron Insley planning issue?" toggle + required $ input + live invoice-preview box. The value persists to `client_tax_plans.discount_applied` (server-gated by member number in `decision.ts`/`pricing.ts`/`extra-meeting.ts`; re-saving with the toggle off clears it). When > 0: the invoice PDF shows gross "Tax Planning Fee" (retainer + implementation + discount), "Discount Applied*" in red, "Net Payable", drops the "(50%)" row suffixes, and adds a small-print footnote; the retainer invoice/receipt email + implementation receipt email get a small-print footnote after the signature. **Purely cosmetic** — charged amounts, receipt PDFs, agreement, and revshare are unchanged; non-discount plans render byte-identical to before.

> **3-way revenue split + Tax Planner allocation gate** (added 2026-07-21). `TaxDecisionForm` + `TaxPricingForm` (shared by program-1 Holistic Tax Priorities + program-4 VFO Tax Planning) now split the fee three ways — **Member / Tax Planner / VFOS** — via a preset `1/3 Member, 1/3 Tax Planner, 1/3 VFOS` or a Custom mode where all three boxes are editable and must sum to `total_fee` (1-cent tolerance, PIPDecisionForm idiom). The pick persists to `member_share` / `tax_planner_share` / `vfos_share`. Strategic-member clients instead get a 4-way split from `src/lib/strategicSplits.js` (`programType='tax'`): Action Coach `{strategic .10, member .30, planner .30, vfos .30}`, Tax Plan IQ `{strategic .10, member .50, planner .20, vfos .20}` (VFOS absorbs the rounding remainder). **Allocation gate:** somebody must be allocated (via the **"Allocate Team Member / Tax Planner"** step — renamed from "Allocate to Advanced Tax Planner" on 2026-08-10 — → `tax_allocate_planner` → `client_tax_plans.tax_planner_id`; **and as of 2026-08-26 a Team Member does NOT satisfy this gate** — they hold the separate `tax_team_member_id` slot, so the v717 "a Team Member satisfies it too" behaviour is gone with the shared column; the *allocation STEP* still goes green on either slot, but this money gate reads `tax_planner_id` alone) before the Yes-path can submit — `decision`(Yes), `pricing`, and `extra-meeting`(Yes) return **400** without a `tax_planner_id`, and the frontend disables submit with "You must allocate a tax planner before submitting." Gotchas #252/#253/#254.

### Decision = `Yes`

> **`risk_mindset` is REQUIRED on this path as of 2026-08-28.** `decision`(Yes) and `pricing` both return **400 "Tax risk mindset is required"** on a blank one, before any write or chain, and both forms block submit with "Please select a tax risk mindset." It was previously written as `fd.taxRiskMindset || null`, so an unanswered dropdown stored NULL and `[TAX_RISK_MINDSET]` shipped **blank** into the agreement PDF — proven live on Lana Hurdle (client 159). **`extra-meeting`(Yes) is deliberately NOT guarded** — that step can legitimately be answered before pricing is settled.

1. UPDATEs plan with pricing fields: `tax_decision`, `risk_mindset`, `retainer_amount`, `implementation_amount`, `total_fee`, `split_type`, `member_share`, `tax_planner_share`, `vfos_share`, `discount_applied` (Diron clients only, see note above), `presentation_link`, `meeting_notes`, `sandbox`. *(`extra_cc` is no longer written — the form field is gone; see the Additional Contacts note below.)*

   > **REVISED FEE PROCESS (2026-08-25) — this is where the version is stamped.** On a plan with no retainer money yet (`retainer_status IS NULL`), `buildFeeColumns()` writes `fee_process_version='2026-08-25'` and derives `retainer_amount` / `implementation_amount` / `initial_retainer_amount` / `final_retainer_amount` from the ONE `totalFee` input — the form may no longer send retainer and implementation amounts, and a request that does is refused with a message naming the mistake. Same stamp on the Step 3b pricing and Step 3c extra-meeting writes. A legacy row reproduces the exact pre-2026-08-25 write above. See [tax-fee-process.md](tax-fee-process.md).
2. **Chains** `automation_TAX_sendagreement` — server-to-server via HTTP fetch + **admin auth token forwarded in body.token** (critical — see Step 4 chain auth note).

### Decision = `Undecided`

1. UPDATEs plan with: `tax_decision='Undecided'`, `potential_tax_savings`, `initial_retainer_quoted`, `tax_token` (32-byte hex generated if not already present), `presentation_link`, `meeting_notes`, `sandbox`. *(no `extra_cc` — see below.)*
   > **Both money fields are `[$,\s]`-stripped before parsing (2026-08-27, v798 — gotcha #457).** `potentialTaxSavings` used a bare `parseFloat` while the fee beside it went through `parseFeeTotal`, so an admin typing **`$757,805`** produced `NaN` → `|| null` at this write and `|| 0` at the `[TAX_SAVINGS]` token, where zero renders the intentional **`"TBD"`** placeholder. The email said *"$TBD"* and the column was NULL, with no error anywhere and the retainer figure perfect. A module-level `parseMoney` now feeds both; `TaxDecisionForm` strips the same characters FE-side as a convenience, **not** as the guard. Plan 90 (Dave Clark) was repaired by SQL and its already-minted draft corrected by hand.
2. Fetches the static **Tax Planning Engagement Agreement PDF** from Supabase Storage public URL `https://ejpsprsmhpufwogbmxjv.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/tax-agreements/tax-planning.pdf` (no auth required). When `member_paying_on_behalf=true` it instead uses the member-paid variant `tax-agreements/tax-planning-member.pdf` (code falls back to `tax-planning.pdf` if missing). **Since 2026-08-25 a quote above $30,000 attaches the 3-payment variant instead — `tax-planning-3pay.pdf` or `tax-planning-member-3pay.pdf`, with a fallback chain down to the 2-payment file.** All four sample PDFs were **re-rendered from `agreement_templates` rows 25–28 and re-uploaded on 2026-09-24** (at the agreement switch-over, verified by text extraction), so they carry the collaborating-team sentence and the Additional Benefits section — see Step 4.
3. Loads `email_templates` row `'TAX_decision_undecided'`.
4. Builds `[BUTTONS]` HTML — 3 buttons (Yes / No / Extra Meeting) pointing to `https://vfoportal.com/tax-decide?token=<tax_token>&decision=<choice>`. Same green/red/blue styling as MAP1's `[BUTTONS]`.
5. Substitutes `[Client Name]`, `[Client First]`, `[Meeting Attendees]` (= "[PF Name] and [Member Name]"), `[Member Name]`, `[PF Name]`, `[TAX_SAVINGS]`, `[INITIAL_RETAINER]`, `[BUTTONS]`, `[PRESENTATION_LINK]`.
6. Builds **multipart MIME** Gmail draft to client with the PDF attached as `Tax-Planning-Engagement-Agreement.pdf`.
   - MIME headers built without empty-string CC/BCC lines (see "MIME-empty-line bug" gotcha).
7. Flips `tax_decision_email_sent='Yes'` AND writes `tax_decision_email_sent_at=now()`. Both columns must be set together — the boolean-flag is the idempotency guard, the timestamp is the base for the **2-business-day** reminder + **4-business-day** PF-notification timers driven by `tax-revshare-sweep-daily` (both tiers count Mon–Fri UTC only — 2026-08-14).

### Decision = `No`

1. UPDATEs plan with `tax_decision='No'`, `presentation_link`, `meeting_notes`, `sandbox`. *(no `extra_cc` — see below.)*

> **Extra Cc left this form on 2026-08-20 (v771).** `TaxDecisionForm`'s "Additional CC recipients" chip list is gone, `tax/decision.ts` no longer writes `client_tax_plans.extra_cc`, and every TAX handler that used to read it now reads the client's **Additional Contacts** instead (`client_contacts.cc_on_emails`, set on the client profile). The reads were **REPLACED, not merged** — merging would have double-Cc'd every backfilled address — and `utils/extra-cc.ts` was deleted. The column is dormant, kept only so past submissions stay auditable. Handlers also fold greeting-flagged contacts into the `[Client First]` **body** token, with an explicit subject-guard in `request-returns` / `request-additional-info`. The planner-facing **assess-reminder** tiers in `revshare-sweep.ts` are deliberately NOT wired — those emails go to the planner, not the client. **And since 2026-08-27 (v798) neither the Cc nor the `CLIENT` role token rides on a `member_paying_on_behalf` send at all** — both `decision.ts` branches, and 24 other sites, pass `CLIENT: undefined` plus an empty `extraCc` when the member pays, so the client is on neither To nor Cc; the **greeting is unaffected**, which is why the `loadAdditionalContacts` call stays (it also returns `greetingFirsts`). Full mechanism → [additional-contacts.md](additional-contacts.md).
2. Loads template `'TAX_decision_decline'`.
3. Substitutes `[Client Name]`, `[Client First]`, `[Meeting Attendees]` (= "[PF Name] and [Member Name]"), `[Member Name]`, `[PF Name]`, `[PRESENTATION_LINK]` (clickable anchor, or "(no link provided)").
4. Creates Gmail draft to client.
5. Flips `tax_decision_email_sent='Yes'`.

**Tables read:** `client_tax_plans`, `clients`, `members`, `pipeline_sandbox_config`, `email_templates`.
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans`.
**External calls:** Supabase Storage public-URL fetch (Undecided only — PDF), Gmail drafts.
**Chains:** `automation_TAX_sendagreement` (Yes only).

> **Chain auth gotcha:** the chain forwards `body.token` (the admin session token from frontend `callApi`) in the chain body, NOT just the Authorization header. The frontend's `callApi` sends `Authorization: Bearer <SUPABASE_ANON_KEY>` and the admin session token in body.token — the auth gate reads token from the body. Forgetting this returns 401 from the chained handler.

---

## Step 3a — Client clicks decision button on `/tax-decide` (Undecided path only)

**Trigger:** Client receives the email, clicks one of the 3 buttons. Browser navigates to [TaxDecidePage.jsx](src/pages/TaxDecidePage.jsx) at `/tax-decide?token=<tax_token>&decision=Yes|No|ExtraMeeting`. **Opening the link records NOTHING** — the page validates the params and renders a confirmation card; the client must click its button to submit (2026-07-27, gotcha #290).

**Handler:** [`automation_TAX_finaldecision`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/final-decision.ts) — PUBLIC handler (pre-auth), called via raw fetch from `/tax-decide` on the confirm button's `onClick`. Handler, body and token are unchanged.

**What it does:**
1. Looks up plan by `tax_token`. If `tax_final_decision` already set, returns `existing_decision` (idempotent).
2. UPDATEs `tax_final_decision`.
3. Branches:
   - **`Yes`**: inserts the `TAX_client_decided_yes` `notifications` row (pipeline='TAX', title `"X chose to proceed with tax planning"`, action-required) — to the assigned PF on a classic plan, **to the allocated Tax Planner + Team Member on a DIRECT plan** via `notifyAllocatedPlanner` (2026-09-21, #521: the pricing form it asks for is the planner's on Direct). The pricing form must then be completed.
   - **`ExtraMeeting`**: inserts the `TAX_client_extra_meeting_requested` row (`"X requested extra meeting (tax)"`), routed the same way.
   - **`No`**: loads template `'TAX_decision_decline'`, drafts Gmail decline email to client. No notification.

**Tables read:** `client_tax_plans`, `clients`, `members`, `pipeline_sandbox_config`, `email_templates`.
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans` (tax_final_decision), `notifications` (Yes/ExtraMeeting).
**Chains:** none — admin still needs to submit pricing / extrameeting outcome manually.

---

## Step 3b — PCADMIN pricing (Undecided → Yes path)

**Trigger:** After client picks Yes via `/tax-decide`, admin sees the notification, opens the client, in the AI PC Admin task (Tax 3) a pricing form auto-appears. Admin fills retainer/implementation/total/split/member/VFOS shares + risk mindset, submits. **Risk mindset is REQUIRED here as of 2026-08-28** — a blank one is refused with 400 "Tax risk mindset is required" before the notifications-cleared write, the plan update and the `sendagreement` chain (see the Step 3 note above for why).

**Handler:** [`automation_TAX_pricing`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/pricing.ts) — AUTH handler. **Since 2026-09-21 also planner-callable on a DIRECT plan only** (`TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` + `denyIfPlannerOnClassicPlan`, decision 29): on Direct the allocated planner prices from the planner portal's Tax 3 Automated-steps card; the `TAX_client_decided_yes` ask that leads here goes to both planner slot-holders on Direct (#521). On a Direct plan the fixed `Direct 45/45/10` split is written whatever the form sent; `TaxPricingForm` hides the split select on Direct — and its submit guard had to stop requiring it (#520). Chains `automation_TAX_sendagreement` with the caller's token.

**What it does:**
1. Validates `tax_final_decision='Yes'` AND `agreement_sent !== 'Yes'`.
2. Marks all unread admin notifications for this client as read (pipeline='TAX').
3. UPDATEs plan pricing fields.
4. **Chains** `automation_TAX_sendagreement` (admin token forwarded in body.token).

**Tables read:** `client_tax_plans`.
**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans`, `notifications.read=true`.
**Chains:** `automation_TAX_sendagreement`.

---

## Step 3c — PCADMIN extra meeting (Undecided → ExtraMeeting path)

**Trigger:** After client picks ExtraMeeting via `/tax-decide`, admin schedules + holds the meeting (manual), then in AI PC Admin task clicks **Yes — proceed with pricing** (opens pricing form) or **No — decline**.

**Handler:** [`automation_TAX_extrameeting`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/extra-meeting.ts) — AUTH handler. Takes `outcome` (Yes/No) and `form_data` if Yes. **Since 2026-09-21 also planner-callable on a DIRECT plan only** (same gate pair as pricing, decision 29); the `TAX_client_extra_meeting_requested` ask goes to both planner slot-holders on Direct (#521); the Yes outcome writes the `Direct 45/45/10` split on a Direct plan (the third split writer). A plan awaiting this outcome is **not** Complete in the planner portal (`!nextActionOverride`, #522).

**What it does:**
- **Yes outcome:** UPDATEs `tax_via_extra_meeting=true` + all pricing fields. Clears unread notifications. **Chains** `automation_TAX_sendagreement`.
- **No outcome:** UPDATEs `tax_via_extra_meeting=true`, `tax_final_decision='No'` (overrides 'ExtraMeeting'). Drafts decline email (reuses `TAX_decision_decline` template). No chain.

**Tables written:** `client_tax_plans`, `notifications.read=true`.
**Chains:** `automation_TAX_sendagreement` (Yes only).

---

## Step 4 — Send agreement to BoldSign

**Trigger:** Server-to-server chain from `automation_TAX_decision` (Yes), `automation_TAX_pricing`, or `automation_TAX_extrameeting` (Yes outcome). NOT directly user-triggered.

**Handler:** [`automation_TAX_sendagreement`](../../supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/actions/tax/send-agreement.ts) — AUTH handler. **Gate:** it sat in NO `role-gates.ts` list until 2026-09-18 — any authenticated member could send an engagement agreement on any plan by id (#515) — and is now `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` **plus** a Direct-only entry on `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` with its own `denyIfPlannerOnClassicPlan`, because the three sources above chain with the CALLER's token and a planner's token re-enters the gate on this second hop (2026-09-21).

> **THREE-PAYMENT AGREEMENTS (2026-08-25).** On a 3-payment plan the selector picks **`agreement_templates` row 27 (`Client Paying - 3 Payments`)** or **row 28 (`Member Paying - 3 Payments`)** — the retainer line is split into `[INITIAL_RETAINER]` and `[FINAL_RETAINER]` lines. The 2-payment rows **25** and **26** carry the same addendum paragraph, and the document is **four pages**, so **all four rows' `field_map` signature fields sit on page 4**. A body edit and its page fix must land in the SAME statement or live sends place signature fields on blank space (**#439**) — see [../integrations/boldsign.md](../integrations/boldsign.md). `client_signed_at` is now stamped (by `ceo-countersign.ts`, belted by `stripe-customer.ts`); **`boldsign-webhook` is untouched.**

**What it does:**
1. Validates `retainer_amount` is set and `agreement_sent !== 'Yes'`.
2. Loads `agreement_templates` row `(pipeline='TAX', service_level='Tax Planning', payment_plan=<'Single' | 'Three Payment'>, active=true)` **plus `.eq("payer_type", memberPays ? "member" : "client")`** — four live rows: **25** client / Single, **26** member / Single (BoldSign template `3e575f15-...`), **27** client / Three Payment, **28** member / Three Payment. The member rows have a `<!--MC-->...<!--/MC-->` member-contribution block that `send-agreement.ts` **strips at render** so the PDF matches the member field_map coordinates (captured from the stripped PDF). When `member_paying_on_behalf=true`, the **member is BoldSign signer 1** (CEO Anton still signer 2).
3. Renders HTML body with placeholder substitutions: `[CLIENT_NAME]`, `[CLIENT_EMAIL]`, `[MEMBER_NAME]`, `[MEMBER_EMAIL]`, `[TAX_RISK_MINDSET]`, `[TOTAL_FEE]`, `[RETAINER_PAYMENT]`, `[INITIAL_RETAINER]` / `[FINAL_RETAINER]` (3-payment rows only), `[IMPLEMENTATION_FEE]` — then the two collaborating-team tokens `[TAX_PLANNING_TEAM_NAME]` / `[MEMBER_BUSINESS_NAME]` (see the note below).
4. Renders the PDF through `renderHtmlToPdf` (html2pdf.app) with **`{ format: "Letter", margin: 0 }`** — the options every agreement sender and the `agreement_pdf_draft` tool share, so a tool-made PDF is the PDF the client gets.
5. POSTs to `https://api.boldsign.com/v1/document/send` with `EnableSigningOrder=true`, `DisableEmails=true`, two signers (client first, CEO Anton second). Hardcoded `BrandId=f6b2e092-73a4-438e-b786-ebd20e472732`. Form fields built from `agreement_templates.field_map` (7 fields: addr+phone on page 1, ceoSig+ceoDate+clientSig+printName+clientDate on page 4 — coordinates user-supplied).
6. Polls `getEmbeddedSignLink` for client signer (5 retries × 5s).
7. UPDATEs plan: `agreement_sent='Yes'`, `boldsign_doc_id`, `client_signed='No'`, `ceo_signed='No'`, `signed_followup_sent_date=<today>`.
8. Loads template `'TAX_agreementsent|Yes'`, substitutes `[ENGAGEMENT]` with embedded sign-link `<a>` tag.
9. Creates Gmail draft to client (`From: VFO Services <aipc@vfo-services.com>`). CC member + PF + the client's **Additional Contact** Cc list (2026-08-20 — replaced the parsed `extra_cc` read). BCC `aanderson` + `platham`.

> **THE COLLABORATING-TEAM SENTENCE + ADDITIONAL BENEFITS (2026-09-24, decision 14 as amended; rows 25–28).** All four TAX rows share one layout.
> - **The sentence.** The opening paragraph ends *"…fees for our engagement.
